The theoretical insights discussed herein provide the underlying principles and justification towards adopting VNNs over workhorse PCA-based learning pipelines, in applications where covariance matrices are useful descriptors of data structure. We also convey how impact of these foundational advances permeates to \textit{principled} designs and applications of learning methods across broad domains where covariance matrices emerge. Notably, we elucidate the conceptual insights facilitated by VNNs to the specific task of characterizing brain age gap for neurodegenerative conditions using neuroimaging datasets, a timely problem in computational neuroscience. Broader impacts to other application domains are discussed as well.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum Feature Engineering for Credit Default Prediction: When and Why IQP Circuits Help Linear Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10505)
*Menachem Finkelstein,Diana Legziel Levy,Zohar Yakhini,Sarel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IQP量子电路特征可提升信用违约预测的F1，但效果依赖输入特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用违约预测中F1的小幅提升可显著降低金融风险，作者探索IQP电路能否在相同特征预算下产生优于经典基线（含Kernel PCA）的特征。

Method: 使用UCI信用卡违约数据集，选n个属性编码为旋转角，n量子比特IQP电路读回2n个期望值作为新特征，采用五折交叉验证。

Result: 16个IQP特征（8量子比特）使逻辑回归F1从0.462升至0.517（p<0.0001）；Kernel PCA仅0.493；经BH校正仍显著。其他分类器无增益。特征选择方式影响明显：RF重要性引导F1=0.523，最大不相关特征降至0.496。

Conclusion: IQP电路通过线性表达能力放大已有信息结构，而非创造新信息；其增益依赖输入特征选择。

Abstract: Credit default prediction is a tabular classification problem in which modest gains in F1 translate directly into reduced financial exposure. We ask whether Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time (IQP) circuits can produce features that improve a classifier over both its raw classical baseline and Kernel PCA - the strongest unsupervised classical non-linear alternative - at an equal feature budget. The dataset provides 23 financial attributes per client; for an n-qubit circuit we select n of them, encode each as a rotation angle, and read 2n expectation values back out as new features. The motivation for using a quantum circuit is computational: an n-qubit IQP circuit runs in constant depth and encodes feature correlations in a 2^n-dimensional Hilbert space, whereas classical simulation of its exact output statistics scales exponentially in n. Using the UCI Default of Credit Card Clients dataset and five-fold cross-validation, we find that appending 16 IQP features (n = 8 qubits) to a Logistic Regression model raises F1 from 0.462 to 0.517 (+0.055, p < 0.0001). Kernel PCA, the next-best method, reaches only 0.493 at the same feature count; the gap survives Benjamini-Hochberg correction across 12 tests (p = 0.00007). No other classifier - Random Forest, SVM, XGBoost, or k-NN - benefits, which points to a linear-expressivity mechanism rather than a generic improvement. We also show that how the 8 input features are chosen matters: Random Forest importance-guided selection reaches F1 = 0.523, while encoding maximally uncorrelated features drops it to 0.496, demonstrating that the circuit amplifies informative structure rather than creating it from scratch.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Playing Whack-a-Mole with misconceptions about memorization, extraction, and copyright](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09320)
*A. Feder Cooper*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本评论质疑某论文关于微调导致版权书籍记忆/抽取的结论，认为其度量方法无效、缺乏阴性对照、成本未报告，因此不足以支持版权主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近日(潜在)原告就该论文联系作者，试图将其作为正在进行的及未来版权诉讼中的有效证据，作者因此撰文指出该论文结论的效度问题，以防误用。

Method: 逐项审查论文的测量程序与主张：(1)考察其书籍记忆覆盖度指标；(2)考察其诱导记忆的提示流程；(3)检查是否包含排除假阳性的阴性对照实验；(4)检查是否报告实验成本。

Result: 发现其主要结果依赖的覆盖度指标只统计远短于领域标准所认可的记忆证据的序列匹配；提示流程存在将被"抽取"文本泄漏进提示的风险；缺少必要的阴性对照以估计假阳性导致的膨胀；且未报告实验成本这一威胁模型的关键组成。

Conclusion: 该论文关于微调可使用户抽取受版权保护书籍的实质部分、并可替代原作的论点，未被其报告结果所支持，不宜作为版权诉讼的有效证据。

Abstract: After careful review, I'm confident the headline fine-tuning memorization results in Alignment Whack-a-Mole use an invalid measurement procedure. The book memorization coverage metric these headline results depend on counts sequence matches far shorter than what field standards consider valid evidence of memorization, and the prompting procedure used to elicit memorization runs the risk of leaking the text being "extracted" in the prompt. The paper doesn't include the negative-control experiments needed to see how much the results are inflated by false positives: claiming extraction success (and therefore memorization of training data) when matches between generations and training data may be due to other factors. Given these validity issues, the paper's claims that fine-tuning lets users extract substantial portions of copyrighted books, in a form that could substitute for the originals, aren't supported by the reported results. The failure to report the experiments' cost (an important component of the threat model) further compromises the copyright claims. I'm writing this note because, in the last month, (prospective) plaintiffs have reached out to me to ask about this paper. They're looking to cite this work as valid evidence in support of claims in ongoing and potential future copyright litigation.

</details>


### [148] [Democracy Needs Reach: Political Equality, Online Speech, and Algorithmic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09465)
*Etienne Brown*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 民主社会中，影响政治结果的真正能力不仅取决于言论自由，还取决于能否触达受众。本文指出社交媒体推荐算法的"曝光不平等"破坏了政治影响力的机会平等（EOPI），并提出"推荐配额"（recommendation floors）作为对策：每个认证账号每周可获得有限数量政治帖文的保底推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在民主体制下，言论影响政治的能力不只依赖表达权，还依赖接触相关受众的机会。然而，社交媒体的算法化推荐将注意力高度集中于少数已被放大的发声者，同时系统性地边缘化其他声音，从而削弱了政治影响力的机会平等这一核心民主理想。

Method: 借助 Niko Kolodny 的理论框架，论证当前推荐算法制造并维系了非正式的不平等（informal inequalities），即将注意力集中于少数人、边缘化他人。基于此提出"推荐配额"机制：每个认证账号每周可为其最多一定数量的政治帖文获得最低限度的保证推荐。

Result: 论证表明算法曝光不均确实破坏了 EOPI；提出以"推荐配额"来均衡政治言论，使用户能够实现有意义的参与。

Conclusion: 推荐配额只是推动数字公共领域更接近民主理想所需的系统性改革中的一环，但它提供了减少在线政治影响力非正式不平等的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Within democracies, the capacity to influence political outcomes through speech depends not only on the right to express oneself, but also on the opportunity to reach relevant audiences. In this paper, I argue that the unequal distribution of algorithmic reach on social media platforms undermines equality of opportunity for political influence (EOPI), which is a central democratic ideal. Drawing on Niko Kolodny's work, I contend that current recommendation algorithms create and perpetuate informal inequalities by concentrating attention among a small minority of already-amplified speakers while systematically marginalizing others. To address this problem, I propose recommendation floors as a mechanism for equalizing political speech. To help users achieve meaningful participation, each verified account would receive guaranteed minimum recommendation for up to a limited number of political posts per week. Although this measure represents one component of the structural reforms needed to move the digital public sphere closer to democratic ideals, it offers a feasible pathway to reducing informal inequalities in political influence online.

</details>


### [149] [Scalable Oversight for AI in Mental Health: Lessons from 350,000 AI Coaching Conversations between Therapy Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09533)
*Matthew A. Scult,John L. Havlik,Kevin Ramotar,Ethan Goh,Manoj Kanagaraj*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于35万+次对话的AI心理辅导部署经验，提出"预防设计—实时监控—持续临床评估"三层"人在环上"监督框架，替代难以规模化的逐条临床审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界常主张由临床医生审核每一条AI输出以保障心理健康服务安全，但警觉性研究表明该做法在规模化时失效，甚至可能反而降低安全性。

Method: 基于在治疗间隙部署AI辅导工具、覆盖350,000+次对话的实践经验，构建并实施三层human-on-the-loop监督框架：预防性设计、实时监控、持续临床评估；并分析临床审核发现如何驱动迭代改进。

Result: 展示了具体临床审核发现如何推动系统的迭代优化，验证了三层监督框架在真实大规模场景中的可行性与作用。

Conclusion: 逐条人工审核不可持续，应转向分层的人在环上监督；并为心理健康专业人员评估AI系统提供实践建议。

Abstract: Clinician review of every AI output is often proposed as a safeguard in mental healthcare, but vigilance research suggests this approach fails at scale and may paradoxically reduce safety. Drawing on our experience deploying an AI coaching tool across 350,000+ conversations between therapy sessions, we describe how we arrived at a three-layer human-on-the-loop oversight framework combining preventive design, real-time monitoring, and continuous clinician evaluation. We show how specific findings from clinical review drove iterative improvements, and offer practical recommendations for mental health professionals evaluating AI systems.

</details>


### [150] [Watermarks Without Verification: AI Text Watermarking After the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09604)
*Alexander Nemecek,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论点是：AI文本水印的真正治理失败不在于水印本身，而在于厂商的保证与用户的质疑都"不可验证"。作者通过开源SynthID-Text在两个开源模型上的实测，展示可验证评估的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年8月EU AI Act第50条生效，Anthropic、Google等厂商被迫在生成文本中默认嵌入水印，引发用户对质量、隐私与可移除性的争议。但双方论断均无法被独立核实，这一"不可验证性"才是核心治理问题。

Method: 将争议性主张按"验证所需条件"分类；在无法测试已部署系统的现实约束下，改用开源SynthID-Text实现在两个开源权重模型上进行评估，分别测试散文与代码场景的水印效果、检测能力及质量代价。

Result: 散文上，水印影响不超过更换采样种子带来的差异；代码上，一个模型正确率下降3个点，另一个模型低于可测量阈值，而检测率接近随机——说明这是"可检测性"而非"质量"的局限。

Conclusion: 剩余验证缺口源于访问受限与制度缺失。作者将其映射为五项要求：发布匹配输出、披露配置、认证审计、共享评估协议以及可互操作的检测机制。

Abstract: On August 2, 2026, the obligations of Article 50 of the EU AI Act took effect, requiring generative AI providers to mark the content their systems produce and ensure it can be detected as AI-generated. Days later, Anthropic disclosed that every Claude model released after that date embeds a watermark based on SynthID-Text in all generated text, enabled by default with no user opt-out; Google has deployed SynthID-Text in Gemini since 2024. Users objected that the watermark degrades quality, particularly for code, that it secretly encodes identifying information, and, in mutual contradiction, that it is easily removable and inescapable; the vendor answered with assurances of unchanged quality, no identifying information, and robustness to light editing. In this work, we argue that neither the objections nor the assurances can currently be verified and that this unverifiability, rather than watermarking itself, is the substantive governance failure. We sort the contested assertions by what it would take to settle each and evaluate the open-source SynthID-Text implementation on two open-weight models, because no public tool can test the deployed systems. On prose, the measured effect of the watermark does not exceed that of changing the sampling seed. On code, the cost is three points of correctness on one model and below measurement on the other, while detection remains near chance, a limitation of detectability rather than quality. The remaining gaps trace to withheld access or missing institutions and we map each to a requirement: release of matched outputs, configuration disclosure, accredited audits, a shared evaluation protocol, and interoperable detection.

</details>


### [151] [Who You Are Adds Nothing Detectable to Where You Go Next: Sociodemographic Conditioning in LLM Next-Location Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09609)
*Xin Wang,Paraic Carroll,Kerry Nice,Sachith Seneviratne,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过构建包含5000名深圳居民的闭环基准，检验人口统计属性对LLM下一位置预测的增量价值，发现其无显著增益，而候选位置构造方式对性能影响巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于个体下一位置预测及旅行模拟，人口统计条件设定常见，但其增量预测价值尚不明确，需直接检验。

Method: 将人口统计记录与5000名深圳居民的被动感知移动数据链接，构建闭环基准（从100个候选目的地中排序），在固定移动历史、候选集和提示内容的前提下，对比有无年龄、性别、职业、收入的条件。

Result: 四种历史长度下top-1准确率配对变化仅-0.8至+0.5个百分点，无显著增益，且在不同设定、两个额外LLM和监督重排器中一致；扰动属性会降低准确率而正确匹配不提升，反向预测中轨迹可恢复收入（AUC 0.708）；候选构造影响更大，去除距离在邻近采样下提升7.7个百分点但在流行度采样下降低22.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 人口统计关联不等于增量预测有用性；下一位置预测的准确率高度依赖候选替代项的构造方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for individual next-location prediction, while sociodemographic conditioning is common in LLM-based travel simulation. Yet the incremental predictive value of sociodemographic attributes remains unclear. To directly test this contribution, sociodemographic records were linked with passively sensed mobility data from 5,000 Shenzhen residents to construct a closed-set benchmark in which models rank 100 candidate destinations. Each prediction instance is evaluated with and without age, gender, occupation and income, while holding mobility history, candidates and all other prompt content fixed. Results show that across four history lengths, the paired change in top-1 accuracy ranges from -0.8 to +0.5 percentage points, with no detectable gain from attributes. This result remains consistent when stay history is withheld, across alternative prediction times, in two additional LLMs and in a supervised reranker trained on the same benchmark. The null does not reflect a lack of model responsiveness to demographic information, as permuted attributes reduce LLM accuracy whereas correctly matched attributes do not improve it. A further asymmetry emerges in the reverse predictive direction, as pre-cut mobility trajectories recover income with an AUC of 0.708, while sociodemographic attributes contribute little to next-location prediction. Beyond demographic conditioning, candidate construction exerts a much larger influence on reported performance. Removing distance raises top-1 accuracy by 7.7 percentage points under proximity sampling but lowers it by 22.3 points under popularity sampling, with the reversal reproduced across all three LLMs. These results distinguish demographic association from incremental predictive usefulness and show that sampled next-location accuracy depends strongly on how candidate alternatives are constructed.

</details>


### [152] [With a Thermomix You Lose the Ability to Cook: A Kitchen Machine Analogy for Applications of Generative AI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09856)
*Nikol Rummel,Valentina Nachtigall,Ernesto Panadero*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过"美善品(Thermomix)智能厨具"类比，探讨生成式AI在教育中的风险与机遇，并借助ICAP与SAMR框架说明不同使用方式对学习参与度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT等生成式AI工具在教育领域引发激烈争论，学界亟需一个概念性视角来审视其风险、机遇及研究方式。

Method: 采用类比方法，将美善品智能厨具的使用情境映射到生成式AI学习案例，并将其置于ICAP与SAMR两个教育框架中加以分析。

Result: 不同模式的工具使用既可支持、也可能削弱学习者的有意义参与与学习效果。

Conclusion: 核心问题不在于学习者是否使用AI，而在于使用方式如何塑造其学习过程；该隐喻为研究者和实践者提供了批判性审视并有效引导生成式AI融入教育实践的概念工具。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative AI tools such as ChatGPT has sparked intense debate about their risks and opportunities for education, as well as the ways researchers should investigate them. In this paper, we approach these discussions through an analogy with the Thermomix, a smart kitchen appliance that has similarly provoked both enthusiasm and critique. By mapping Thermomix use cases onto examples of learning with generative AI, and situating them within the ICAP and SAMR frameworks, we show how different modes of tool use can either support or undermine meaningful engagement and learning. The Thermomix metaphor underscores that the central question is not whether learners employ AI, but how such use shapes their learning processes. In doing so, we provide a conceptual lens for researchers and practitioners to critically examine - and more effectively guide - the integration of generative AI into educational practice.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Training: A Feasibility Taxonomy for Inference-Time AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10105)
*Samar Ansari*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出推理阶段计算治理的可行性分类学：评估20种推理时机制，发现15种已有商用技术基础，但仅对合作部署者和低-中能力用户有效，对高能力国家行为者均不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行计算治理以训练为对象（阈值、报告要求、前沿AI制度均基于训练算力，将训练后模型视为监管单元），但能力正通过推理时扩展、智能体脚手架、消费硬件压缩等方式向部署阶段迁移，该图景不完整，需研究监管对象从训练运行转向推理调用后可用的机制。

Method: 建立涵盖监控、验证、执行三类共20种推理时机制的可行性分类学，基于四家供应商的文献证据按四级成熟度量表评级；用二维对手模型（三能力层级×四对手角色）进行压力测试，并将各机制映射至四种治理情景（国内监管、双边/多边协调、行业自律、算力市场治理）；开展替代性分析连接姊妹硬件论文；对随机子集评级做第二评分者信度检验。

Result: 20种机制中15种已有在产商业技术基础，但治理级保证与对抗鲁棒性差异显著。对抗分析显示，该成熟度仅对合作部署者和低-中能力用户成立；无机制对高能力国家行为者达到充分评级；微调会移除执行簇中的模型内部组件，但平台外部控制仍可存续。第二评分者信度检验（二次加权Cohen's kappa）为0.74。

Conclusion: 推理阶段治理机制技术上部分可行但保证水平有限，且对强对抗者脆弱；提出条件性替代原则，描述推理阶段与硬件阶段机制在既定条件下可提供可比监管覆盖，为超越"训练中心"范式的计算治理提供框架与局限边界。

Abstract: Compute governance today is a governance of training: the thresholds, reporting requirements, and frontier-AI regimes now in force attach to training compute and treat the trained model as the regulatory unit. That picture is incomplete: capability increasingly migrates to the deployment stage through inference-time scaling, agentic scaffolding, and compression onto consumer hardware. This paper asks which mechanisms are available once the regulatory object shifts from the training run to the inference call. We develop a feasibility taxonomy of twenty inference-time mechanisms across monitoring, verification, and enforcement, each rated on a four-point readiness scale against a documented four-vendor evidence base. We then stress the taxonomy against a two-dimensional adversary model (three capability tiers crossed with four adversary roles) and map each mechanism to four governance scenarios (domestic regulation, bilateral or multilateral coordination, industry self-regulation, and compute-marketplace governance). Fifteen of the twenty mechanisms have commercial technical substrates in production today, although governance-grade assurance and adversarial robustness vary substantially. The adversary analysis shows that this readiness holds only against a cooperative deployer and a low-to-medium-capability user: no mechanism rates adequate against a high-capability state-level deployer, and fine-tuning removes the model-internal components of the enforcement cluster, although platform-external controls can persist. A substitution analysis connects the taxonomy to a companion hardware paper as a conditional substitution principle describing when inference-stage and hardware-stage mechanisms provide comparable regulatory coverage under stated conditions. A second-rater reliability check on a random subset of the readiness ratings returned a quadratic-weighted Cohen's kappa of 0.74.

</details>


### [154] [Senseful Consense: Towards Simplified Cookie Banners using Plain Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10271)
*Minela Bećirović,Ha Dao,Mannat Kaur,Martin Johns,Alexandra Dirksen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该可行性研究分析200个网站的Cookie横幅文本，用AI映射到IAB TCF标准处理目的，验证用"简单语言"改写可把理解难度从大学水平降到七年级水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR与ePrivacy要求同意信息清晰可及，但多数Cookie横幅充斥术语、表述含糊、内容过多或过少，用户难以理解。

Method: 取200个网站的Cookie横幅文本，用AI将抽取内容映射归类到标准化的处理目的；以简化描述替换复杂法律术语，并比较改写前后的可读性等级。

Result: 成功将理解门槛从大学毕业生水平降至七年级水平；但简单语言的效果受原始内容信息量限制，无法弥补缺失法律必要细节的横幅。

Conclusion: 简单语言是数字无障碍的重要工具，但需与标准化实施指南配套，才能让Cookie横幅既易读又信息充分。

Abstract: While the GDPR and ePrivacy Directive mandate that consent information must be clear and accessible, most modern cookie banners remain obscured by technical jargon, vague phrasing, and frequent content overload or underload. This feasibility study investigates the impact of applying plain language (Einfache Sprache) to cookie banners within the IAB Transparency & Consent Framework (TCF). In our study, we analysed cookie banner texts from 200 websites, using AI-based mapping to categorise extracted content into standardised processing purposes. By substituting complex legal terms with simplified descriptions, we successfully demonstrated that the comprehension barrier can be lowered from a college-graduate level to a 7th-grade level. However, the effectiveness of plain language is inherently constrained by the informativeness of the original content; it cannot compensate for banners that omit legally required details. We conclude that while plain language is a vital tool for digital accessibility, it must be paired with standardised implementation guidelines to ensure that cookie banners are both readable and informative.

</details>


### [155] [Total Simulated Survey Error: Designing and Diagnosing Survey Responses from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10280)
*Indira Sen,Georg Ahnert,Leah von der Heyde,Jana Lasser,Bernd Weiß,Markus Strohmaier*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出TS2E（Total Simulated Survey Error）框架，用于系统识别、追踪和记录LLM生成问卷回答中的误差与偏差，为调查模拟设计者提供端到端的统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被视为"硅样本"，可替代真人回答问卷以估计舆论、设计政策或作为社会科学数据，但其社会偏见、泛化性及技术局限等问题尚不清楚，且模拟设计空间庞大，缺乏系统性理解。作者因此追问：如何系统识别、追踪和记录LLM生成问卷回答中的局限？

Method: 借鉴定量社会科学传统（尤其调查方法论与测量理论），构建TS2E框架，枚举在调查模拟生命周期不同阶段可能出现的概念性错误与系统性偏差，并通过理论加实证的案例研究加以说明。

Result: 框架提供了关于LLM生成调查数据的统一、端到端视角，可用于分析不同设计选择的影响（衔接多元宇宙分析思路），并结合案例展示了误差识别的可行性。

Conclusion: TS2E框架帮助调查模拟设计者系统识别并反思LLM生成问卷中的误差，为提升LLM作为"硅样本"的效度提供方法论工具。

Abstract: Large Language models (LLMs), having been trained on vast amounts of human-generated data, may encode the attitudes and behaviors of these humans. As such, LLMs show promise in mimicking human-like patterns that facilitate their use in simulating people in a wide variety of contexts. One such context is using LLMs as 'silicon samples', i.e., proxies of people in answering survey questions to establish public opinion, design policies, or use as (social) scientific data. However, several critical questions of social biases, generalization, and technical limitations remain, further complicated by a vast design space open to simulation designers. Multiverse analyses might help us make sense of the impact of different design choices, however, we lack a systematic understanding of the design space of LLM-generated surveys as well as how these decisions interplay with inherent LLM limitations. Therefore, how do we systematically identify, trace, and document limitations in LLM-generated survey responses? Building on traditions in the quantitative social sciences, specifically survey methodology and measurement theory, we investigate threats to the validity of LLM-generated survey responses. To do so, we design a framework that enumerates conceptual errors and systematic biases that can occur at different stages of the survey simulation lifecycle. Our framework, called the Total Simulated Survey Error (TS2E) Framework, provides a unified and end-to-end perspective on LLM-generated survey data. The framework, illustrated through a theoretical and empirical case study, enables survey simulation designers to systematically identify and reflect on errors in LLM-generated surveys.

</details>


### [156] [Emergency Department Revisit Quality Review Screening: Exploring Human Decision-Making and Artificial Intelligence Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10421)
*Jonathan A. Handler,Marlene I. Robles-Granda,Jacob E. Mefford,Jeremy S. McGarvey,Gregory S. Podolej,Colleen J. Klein,Matthew D. Dalstrom,William F. Bond*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究探索ED再就诊的病因诊断对筛查，发现GPT-4与临床医生评分相关性差，但基于LLM知识图谱的KGA算法对临床医生认为需进一步评估的诊断对达到83-100%阳性预测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ED再就诊常用于质量审查，但通常仅限48-72小时内以控制病历审查负担，可能遗漏质量改进机会。作者希望扩展筛查范围同时不显著增加审查工作量。

Method: 回顾性探索性研究，随机抽取多医院系统中1-14天内同系统再就诊的ED病例。仅依据每次就诊的主诊断，由2-3名临床医生与GPT-4 LLM评估诊断对的"目标"（是否值得进一步评估）。基于评分分析，构建利用LLM填充知识图谱的KGA算法自动筛查可疑诊断对。

Result: 纳入99个诊断对。GPT-4与临床医生评分相关性差，将94%的诊断对评为需随访（比临床医生高4.4-13.3倍）。临床医生中，再就诊医疗严重度与目标显著相关；鉴别诊断/并发症复合指标仅未调整分析显著。KGA对至少一名临床医生判定需进一步评估达到83-100%阳性预测值。

Conclusion: 结果可为改进ChatGPT等LLM筛查提供方向。需进一步研究验证KGA能否在不显著增加审查负担的情况下扩大筛查范围与产出。

Abstract: Background: Emergency Department (ED) return visits are commonly reviewed for quality assurance, but are often limited (e.g., to revisits within 48-72 hours) to increase actionable finding yield while minimizing chart review burden. Those limitations may lead to missed quality improvement opportunities.
  Methods: We conducted an exploratory, retrospective study of randomly selected ED visits to a multihospital health system having an ED revisit within 1-14 days to the same health system. Given only each visit's primary diagnosis, raters (2-3 clinicians and GPT-4 large language model [LLM]) assessed characteristics of the diagnosis pairs, including the "target": whether a pair warranted further assessment. Informed by rater response analyses, an algorithm leveraging an LLM-populated knowledge graph ("KGA") was created to automatically screen for potentially concerning pairs, then preliminarily assessed.
  Results: 99 diagnosis pairs were included. GPT-4 responses poorly correlated to clinician raters, rating nearly all (94%) pairs as warranting follow-up (4.4-13.3 times more than clinicians). However, prompt engineering was minimal. Among clinician raters, revisit medical gravity was consistently significantly associated with the target, while a differential diagnosis/complication composite was significantly associated on unadjusted, but not adjusted (though less powered) analysis. The KGA achieved 83-100% positive predictive value for at least one clinician rater determining further assessment was warranted based on the diagnosis pair.
  Conclusion: These results can inform next steps for improving screening with LLMs like ChatGPT. Further research is warranted to validate this preliminary work's finding that the KGA may enable enhancing the scope and yield of screening without substantially increasing reviewer workload.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Support Discovery With Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares for Fixed-Charge Network Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09295)
*Sindura Saraswathi,Christian Kümmerle*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于IRLS的连续优化算法求解大规模单商品固定费用网络流问题，结合平滑Lasry-Lions代理、半光滑牛顿子问题求解与支撑集搜索，在410个实例上取得最佳目标质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCNFP将连续流量分配与离散弧激活决策耦合，是网络设计与资源分配的经典难题。精确MILP虽忠实刻画固定费用结构，但在大规模网络上求解困难，需要可扩展的替代方法。

Method: 采用迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）框架，用平滑非凸Lasry-Lions代理替代不连续的固定费用与线性弧成本目标，求解一系列加权二次流量子问题；每个子问题用热启动对偶半光滑牛顿法求解，其牛顿系统具加权图拉普拉斯结构，可借助现代拉普拉斯求解器。另提出引入目标驱动扰动重启与锚点并集受限搜索的变体，联合利用IRLS与互补启发式发现的支撑集。

Result: 在410个基准、合成及大规模实例上，所提方法在可扩展FCNFP算法中目标质量最佳，相对限时MILP参考的平均差距为1.316%，在非MILP方法中胜或平率达90.0%。

Conclusion: 将平滑连续优化与支撑集级别搜索相结合，是生成大规模FCNFP高质量可行解的有效策略。

Abstract: The fixed-charge network flow problem (FCNFP) couples continuous flow allocation with discrete arc-activation decisions, making it a canonical but computationally challenging model for a variety of network design and resource allocation problems. Exact mixed-integer linear programming formulations capture the fixed-charge structure faithfully, but often become difficult to solve on large networks. We propose a scalable continuous-optimization algorithm for large-scale single-commodity FCNFP based on an iteratively reweighted least-squares (IRLS) framework. The method replaces the discontinuous fixed-charge and linear arc cost objective with a smooth nonconvex Lasry--Lions surrogate and solves a sequence of weighted quadratic flow subproblems. Each subproblem is solved by a warm-started dual semismooth Newton method whose Newton systems have weighted graph-Laplacian structure, enabling the use of modern Laplacian solvers. To further improve the discovered arc supports of the challenging underlying combinatorial problem, we also develop an algorithmic variant that incorporates objective-driven perturbation restarts and an anchor-union restricted search that jointly leverages supports discovered by IRLS and by complementary FCNFP heuristics. Computational experiments on 410 benchmark, synthetic, and large-scale instances show that our method obtains the best objective quality among the evaluated scalable FCNFP algorithms, with a mean gap of $1.316\%$ to a time-limited MILP reference and a win-or-tie rate of $90.0\%$ among the non-MILP methods. The results indicate that combining smooth continuous optimization with support-level search is an effective strategy for producing high-quality feasible solutions to large-scale FCNFP.

</details>


### [158] [Communication-efficient ADMM over Hierarchical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09361)
*Binh Nguyen,Shuangqing Wei,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出hADMM算法，基于ADMM求解树状网络上的分层优化问题，通过查询-响应通信机制显著提升通信效率，并保证凸性下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ADMM的分层优化方法通信成本高，亟需提升树状网络上的通信效率。

Method: 基于ADMM，通过重排增广拉格朗日函数建立节点间查询-响应通信机制，显式利用分层树结构。

Result: 在凸性假设下保证渐近收敛；基于线性矩阵不等式分析收敛率，证明温和条件下达线性收敛；三个数值实验显示兼容任意树结构且通信效率优于现有方法。

Conclusion: hADMM在树状网络分层优化中兼顾通信效率与收敛性能，优于现有ADMM方法。

Abstract: This paper develops a novel distributed optimization algorithm based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to solve hierarchical optimization problems over tree-structured networks, termed hierarchical ADMM (hADMM), with a particular focus on enhancing communication efficiency across the network. By rearranging the augmented Lagrangian to establish a query-response communication mechanism between nodes that explicitly exploits the hierarchical tree structure, the proposed algorithm significantly reduces communication costs compared to existing ADMM-based methods for hierarchical optimization. Furthermore, hADMM guarantees asymptotic convergence under convexity assumptions. We also present a convergence rate analysis based on linear matrix inequalities to characterize the maximum theoretically achievable convergence rates across different network topologies, showing that the proposed hADMM attains linear convergence under mild conditions. Three numerical experiments demonstrate that hADMM is compatible with arbitrary tree network structures and outperforms existing approaches in terms of communication efficiency.

</details>


### [159] [Powell-Style Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization with Complexity Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09441)
*Abraar Chaudhry,Katya Scheinberg,Scholar Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Powell风格的基于模型信赖域无导数算法变体，利用低阶多项式插值并维护插值集几何，推导复杂度界并扩展到随机子空间与噪声情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Powell最初提出并实现的基于模型信赖域无导数方法缺乏严格复杂度分析，尤其其几何维护机制尚未被完整纳入理论分析，且随机子空间与噪声场景下的表现有待研究。

Method: 提出Powell方法变体，基于低阶多项式插值并完整融入Powell的插值集几何处理策略；在随机生成子空间中应用这些方法，并扩展子空间模型信赖域方法分析至带噪声函数评估情形。

Result: 推导出使这些方法在理论上与其他无导数方法具有竞争力的复杂度界；随机子空间应用得到接近紧的复杂度；并进行了大量数值比较，连接实际与理论性能。

Conclusion: 该分析将Powell几何处理纳入理论框架，扩展了子空间方法至噪声场景，并通过数值实验验证了理论与实践性能的一致性。

Abstract: We propose variants of model-based trust region derivative-free algorithms that are closest to methods initially proposed and implemented by Powell. These methods rely on low degree polynomial interpolation and carefully maintain geometry of the interpolation sets. We are able to derive complexity bounds for these methods that make them theoretically competitive to other derivative-free methods. Applying these methods in randomly generated subspaces recovers what we believe to be nearly tight complexity. This paper builds on recent results where complexity of a much simplified version of Powell's methods was derived. Here, we extend the analysis to fully incorporate Powell's geometry handling approach and conduct an extensive numerical comparison of the model-based trust region methods connecting practical and theoretical performance. We also extend the analysis of subspace model-based trust region methods to the case of noisy function evaluations.

</details>


### [160] [Afterimage Slow Regions in First-Order Methods for Linear Conic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09449)
*Shucheng Kang,Heng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出"慢区域"（slow region）概念，用于刻画一阶锥规划方法在状态空间中的何处出现慢收敛，并通过"残像原理"构造此类区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多从时间点或问题实例角度分析慢收敛，本文转而关注状态空间中慢收敛出现的位置，以更本质地理解长平台现象。

Method: 针对参数化平均不动点迭代，提出慢区域族：在慢区域上，每步仅将状态移动至到不动点集距离的一小部分。进一步提出"残像原理"，通过构造共享极限参数的中心族与花瓣族，使残差场接近但不动点集或前向漂移相距甚远，从而认证慢区域。

Result: 验证了ADMM、sGS-ADMM与PDHG满足假设，并给出LP、SOCP、SDP实例展示慢区域的多样性。

Conclusion: 慢区域视角为理解锥规划一阶方法的慢收敛提供了新的状态空间几何工具。

Abstract: First-order methods for linear conic programming often stall on long plateaus. Existing analyses characterize when during a run or on which problem instances slow convergence occurs; we instead ask where in the state space slow convergence is present. We introduce the slow region family for parameterized averaged fixed-point iterations. On a slow region, one step moves the state by only a small fraction of its distance to the fixed-point set, so an orbit starting there keeps almost its initial distance for arbitrarily many iterations. We then develop the afterimage principle to construct them. A center family and a nearby petal family share the same limit parameter, so their residual fields become close, while their fixed-point sets or forward drifts stay far apart. The petal geometry then certifies a slow region for the center. We verify the standing assumptions for ADMM, sGS-ADMM, and PDHG, and give an LP, SOCP, and SDP gallery showing how varied slow regions can be.

</details>


### [161] [Unifying Variational View of Accelerated Primal-Dual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09454)
*Cheng Chang,Mehran Mesbahi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的变分框架，利用Euler-Lagrange-Rayleigh原理从耦合增广Bregman拉格朗日量系统导出仿射约束凸优化中的加速原始-对偶流，涵盖镜像流与ADMM流，给出O(e^{-b_t})收敛保证，并推广到概率分布约束优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速原始-对偶算法多零散发展，缺乏统一变分视角，且现有方法多局限于特定几何或有限维场景。

Method: 对耦合增广Bregman拉格朗日量应用ELR原理，在一般Bregman几何下生成加速原始-对偶动力学，统一已有镜像流与ADMM流。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了O(e^{-b_t})收敛保证，并将框架从有限维优化扩展到概率分布上的约束优化。

Conclusion: 该变分框架统一并推广了加速原始-对偶流，为算法设计提供原理性工具，且可延伸至分布空间。

Abstract: We develop a unifying variational framework for accelerated primal-dual flows in affinely constrained convex optimization. In particular, it is shown that applying the Euler-Lagrange-Rayleigh (ELR) principle to coupled augmented Bregman Lagrangians systematically generates accelerated dynamics over general Bregman geometries and recovers a family of existing primal-dual mirror and Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) flows as special cases. We then establish $\mathcal{O}(e^{-b_t})$ convergence guarantees for these flows under mild assumptions. Lastly, we show that the proposed framework for algorithmic development extends from finite-dimensional optimization to constrained optimization over probability distributions.

</details>


### [162] [Global Optimization Framework for Automated Low-Thrust Gravity-Assist Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09515)
*Ryo Iijima,Kenshiro Oguri,Toshinori Kuwahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出凸剪枝方法，可在宽搜索阶段直接求解带约束的多变量问题，并结合推力正则化的局部低推力优化算法，实现自动化全局探索与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力辅助轨迹设计需搜索大量航段组合，但直接对所有组合做低推力NLP优化计算量过大。传统框架用Lambert等轻量模型做宽搜索，但仅支持低维、约束有限的建模（如每段最多几次脉冲），无法处理低推力设计所需的带约束多变量优化，可能过早剪掉可行轨迹。

Method: 1）引入凸剪枝方法，在保持计算效率的同时，于宽搜索阶段直接求解带约束的多变量问题；2）在宽搜索阶段加入基于推力正则化的鲁棒局部低推力优化算法，二者结合实现全局探索与优化。

Result: 在受BepiColombo启发的含九次引力辅助场景中，该框架成功发现36,335个解，远多于传统方法的4,664个，并将可行发射窗口扩展了一年。

Conclusion: 该框架能在宽搜索阶段处理带约束多变量优化，避免过早剪枝，实现低推力引力辅助轨迹的自动化全局探索与优化，显著提升解的数量与可行发射窗口范围。

Abstract: Gravity-assist trajectory design requires searching numerous leg combinations, but applying nonlinear programming (NLP) for low-thrust trajectory optimization to all combinations is computationally prohibitive. Therefore, conventional frameworks first perform broad search using lightweight trajectory models such as Lambert-based calculation and simplified deep-space maneuver models, while pruning infeasible candidates. However, to maintain efficiency, conventional approaches handle only low-dimensional formulations with limited constraints such as up to a couple of impulse maneuvers per leg. Consequently, they cannot accommodate the constrained multivariable optimization required for low-thrust design, running the risk of prematurely pruning viable trajectories. To address this, this paper introduces a convex pruning approach capable of solving constrained multivariable problems directly within broad search while retaining the computational efficiency. We then augment the broad search stage with a robust local low-thrust optimization algorithm based on thrust regularization, which together enable global exploration and optimization in an automated fashion. We apply the proposed framework in a BepiColombo-inspired scenario involving nine gravity assists, successfully demonstrating its broad search capability to discover a far greater number of solutions (36,335) compared to a conventional approach (4,664) while identifying a wider feasible launch window by one year.

</details>


### [163] [Oracle Complexity of Stochastic Fixed-Point Equations with Nonexpansive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09524)
*Jelena Diakonikolas,Cristóbal Guzmán,David Martínez-Rubio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般范数下、用随机oracle访问非扩张自映射时，计算近似不动点（残差≤ε）的oracle复杂度，给出基于递归锚定技术的算法，并在type-2空间（如ℓ_p, p∈[2,∞]）达到Õ(σ²ε⁻³+ε⁻¹)复杂度，同时给出高维ℓ_∞范数下的近匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不动点计算复杂度结果多针对特定范数（如ℓ_2）或确定性/精确设定，缺乏对一般范数、随机oracle与非扩张映射下统一复杂度刻画，尤其不明确在一般范数空间及稀疏噪声下能否改进复杂度。

Method: 提出基于递归锚定（recursive anchoring）技术的算法，适用于具有弱Rademacher型q>1的任意范数；在type-2空间（如ℓ_p, p∈[2,∞]）分析其高概率oracle复杂度。同时采用对抗/信息论下界论证，证明高维ℓ_∞实例上任意常数成功概率随机算法的近匹配（至多对数因子）下界，并推广到稀疏噪声设定。

Result: 对type-2空间，算法达到随机oracle复杂度Õ(σ²ε⁻³+ε⁻¹)；对高维ℓ_∞范数实例证明近匹配下界（匹配至多对数因子），下界对任意常数成功概率随机算法成立；结果扩展到稀疏噪声情形，即任意ℓ_p范数测度同阶时也无法通过非匹配ℓ_p范数测度方差改进关于ε的复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般范数下随机非扩张不动点问题建立了近乎紧致的oracle复杂度界，表明在type-2空间所给算法近似最优，并排除了通过选择非匹配ℓ_p范数测度方差来改善ε依赖的可能性。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of computing a point with small fixed-point residual $\|T(x)-x\| \leq ε$, for a general norm $\|\cdot\|$ and a self-map $T$ of a compact convex set. We study this problem in the setting where $T$ is nonexpansive with respect to the same norm $\|\cdot\|$ and accessed via an unbiased stochastic oracle with bounded variance $σ^2$. We provide an algorithm that solves such instances for any norm with a weak Rademacher type $q > 1$, with high probability. The algorithm is based on a recursive anchoring technique. For type-$2$ spaces, such as $\ell_p$-spaces for $p \in [2, \infty]$, our algorithm attains stochastic oracle complexity $\tilde O(σ^2 ε^{-3} + ε^{-1})$. We further prove a near-matching lower bound (i.e., matching up to poly-log factors) for such $\ell_{\infty}$-norm instances in high dimensions. Our lower bound holds against any randomized algorithm that succeeds with constant probability. It further extends to settings with ``sparse'' noise, where variance measured with respect to any $\ell_p$ norm is of the same order, ruling out the possibility of improving oracle complexity as a function of $\varepsilon$ by measuring variance in a non-matching $\ell_p$ norm.

</details>


### [164] [A Function-Space Framework for BDDC Preconditioning in Control- and State-Constrained Sparse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09541)
*Harbir Antil,Keegan L. A. Kirk,Umarkhon Rakhimov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为带盒约束控制、L1稀疏与Moreau-Yosida正则化状态约束的椭圆最优控制问题，在无穷维层面构造BDDC预条件子并给出两级加性Schwarz表示与收敛性数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动集半光滑牛顿法产生的界面系统规模大、条件数差，亟需高效可扩展的预条件子，而现有工作多局限于离散层面且缺少理论保障。

Method: 在无穷维层面直接建立区域分解与BDDC构造，消去内部变量得到状态与伴随迹的Schur补；证明全局-局部等价性及局部/部分装配问题的适定性，并给出含独立局部求解与有限维粗解的加性Schwarz表示；用协调有限元实现并测试分布式与Neumann边界控制。

Result: 固定H/h时GMRES迭代数与网格细化几乎无关，仅随H/h适度增长；丰富粗空间显著改善收敛并降低对正则化与稀疏权重的敏感性；状态约束问题中令Moreau-Yosida罚参数正比于h²可获得近恒定的迭代数。

Conclusion: BDDC预条件子在最优控制界面系统上具备网格无关的可扩展性与理论适定性，粗空间设计与罚参数选取是保证收敛稳健的关键。

Abstract: We develop a Balancing Domain Decomposition by Constraints (BDDC) preconditioner for the interface systems arising from active-set semi-smooth Newton linearizations of elliptic optimal control problems with box-constrained controls, $L^1$-sparsity, and Moreau--Yosida regularized state constraints. The domain decomposition and BDDC construction are formulated directly at the infinite-dimensional level. Eliminating the interior variables yields a Schur complement for the state and adjoint traces assembled from local subdomain operators. We prove a global--local equivalence result, establish well-posedness of the local and partially assembled problems under verifiable conditions on the primal constraints. The BDDC operator admits a two-level additive Schwarz representation with independent local solves and a finite-dimensional coarse solve. We implement the preconditioner using conforming finite elements and test it for distributed and Neumann boundary control. For a fixed ratio of subdomain diameter $H$ to mesh size $h$, the GMRES iteration counts are nearly independent of mesh refinement and grow only moderately as this ratio increases. Enriching the coarse space substantially improves convergence and reduces sensitivity to the control regularization and sparsity weights. For the state-constrained problem, scaling the Moreau--Yosida penalty parameter proportionally to $h^2$ yields nearly constant GMRES iteration counts as the mesh is refined.

</details>


### [165] [The Car Resequencing Problem with Makespan Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09602)
*Xinyi Guo,Jean-François Côté*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带返回车道（RL）的汽车重排序问题，目标最小化重排序完工时间，证明NP完全并提出两阶段BP-CP精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车重排序通过中间缓冲区调整两相邻车间间的车辆顺序。带返回车道的新变体中，前进车道与返回车道均为FIFO但方向相反，车辆可循环流动，提升灵活性同时增加复杂度，需研究其最短完成时间。

Method: 证明问题NP完全；设计两阶段分支定价与约束规划集成方法（BP-CP）。第一阶段提出三种主问题（MP）版本，各配一个定义在特定图上的子问题；第二阶段用分支的部分整数解构建约束规划模型，产出可行解或向MP添加可行性割。

Result: BP-CP显著扩大可解生产批次规模，尤其在中度松弛MP下；RL明显增强重排序灵活性，使较小缓冲区能达到与无RL较大缓冲区相当的完工时间。

Conclusion: 仅扩大车道容量对重排序性能提升有限，甚至可能降低效率；返回车道相当于多条前进车道，支持更小且经济的缓冲区设计，同时保持高效JIT运作。

Abstract: Problem definition: The car resequencing problem involves rearranging the sequence of cars between two adjacent production shops via an intermediate buffer. This study explores a new variant that aims to minimize the resequencing makespan when the buffer features several forward lanes and a return lane (RL). Both types of lanes follow first-in-first-out rules, but operate in opposite directions. Through the RL, cars can circulate between forward lanes, thereby increasing resequencing flexibility and complexity. The goal is to position cars over time to complete the target sequence change in the shortest possible time. Methodology/results: We prove the problem is NP-complete and develop an exact two-stage branch-and-price approach integrated with constraint programming (BP-CP). Three versions of the master problem (MP) are proposed for the first stage, each paired with a subproblem defined on a specific graph. Using partial integer solutions from branching, a constraint-programming model built in the second stage yields either a feasible solution to the problem or a feasibility cut added to the MP. Experimental results indicate that BP-CP greatly increases the scale of solvable instances for a production batch, especially with a moderately relaxed MP. A comparative analysis shows that the RL markedly enhances resequencing flexibility, enabling smaller buffers to attain makespans comparable to those of larger buffers without the RL across varying resequencing complexities. Managerial implications: Expanding lane capacity alone yields limited gains in resequencing performance and may even reduce efficiency. The RL acts as several forward lanes, supporting a smaller and cost-effective buffer design while maintaining efficient just-in-time operations.

</details>


### [166] [Primal-Dual Inexact Newton-MR for Nonconvex Optimization with Equality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09683)
*Oscar Smee,Fred Roosta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共轭残差(CR)内层求解器的不精确原始-对偶牛顿法，直接处理子问题的不定性，避免正则化，适用于大规模非凸等式约束优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性等式约束优化问题广泛存在，但经典方法（如SQP、原始-对偶牛顿法）依赖强假设（如Lagrangian Hessian在约束Jacobian零空间上正定），实践中需强正则化或正定替代；且大规模下精确求解牛顿子问题不可行。

Method: 提出不精确原始-对偶牛顿法，内层使用共轭残差(CR)方法。利用CR的负曲率检测、迭代单调性和下降保证等性质，直接处理子问题中出现的不定性，避免对Lagrangian Hessian的有害正则化，并自然支持不精确求解。

Result: 建立了最坏情况全局收敛性保证，并在大规模非凸问题上展现出强实证性能。

Conclusion: 该方法无需强正则化，能有效处理不定性子问题，兼具理论保证与大规模非凸优化的实用性能。

Abstract: Optimization problems with nonlinear equality constraints arise throughout science, engineering, and increasingly in machine learning. Prominent methods for solving such problems include sequential quadratic programming and, more broadly, primal-dual Newton methods. Classical analyses of these methods typically rely on strong assumptions, perhaps most notably positive definiteness of the Lagrangian Hessian on the null space of the constraint Jacobian. In practice, this assumption often necessitates strong regularization or the use of a positive definite Hessian surrogate. Moreover, in large-scale settings, solving the primal-dual Newton subproblem exactly is often computationally infeasible. To address these issues, we propose an inexact primal-dual Newton method with an inner solver based on the conjugate residual (CR) method. Exploiting recently established properties of CR, including negative-curvature detection, iterate monotonicity, and descent guarantees, our method handles indefiniteness in the subproblem directly as it arises. Our method thereby avoids detrimental regularization of the Lagrangian Hessian while naturally accommodating inexact solves. We establish worst-case global convergence guarantees and demonstrate strong empirical performance on large-scale, nonconvex problems.

</details>


### [167] [Counterexamples for BFGS-type methods under arbitrary strong Wolfe constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09686)
*Rui Diao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文解决了自Dai (2002)以来悬而未决的公开问题：对任意Armijo参数c1∈(0,1)，是否存在使BFGS类方法不收敛的光滑非凸函数。作者构造了C^∞、下有界、梯度Lipschitz的目标函数，使BFGS、L-BFGS、Broyden正族及HS共轭梯度法在标准线搜索下梯度范数恒为1，永不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dai (2002)的反例仅局限于很小的Armijo参数(c1≤1/84或c1≤69/7480)，且目标函数无下界，无法回答是否存在对每个c1∈(0,1)都成立的普遍性反例，此问题由Dai在Gilbert讨论后提出，长期未解。

Method: 对任意给定线搜索参数0<c1<c2<1，在最小可能维度n=2上构造目标函数f∈C^∞(R^2)，下有界且梯度Lipschitz连续。利用平面内非衰减的共轭下降轨道，配合显式管状插值构造，其双峰轴向曲率剖面可将Armijo比值置于(0,1)任意位置。适用于一大类共轭方法C，包括全记忆BFGS、任意记忆m≥1的L-BFGS、Broyden正族和Hestenes-Stiefel共轭梯度法。

Result: 构造的函数使C类中每个方法以第一局部极小点线搜索生成无限迭代序列，满足‖∇f(x_k)‖=1对所有k≥0。步长同时满足强/弱Wolfe、Armijo和Goldstein条件，常数取(c1,c2)。

Conclusion: 这一结果彻底解决了Dai提出的公开问题，表明在现实线搜索参数下，BFGS及其多种变体原则上可对光滑非凸函数（甚至下有界、梯度Lipschitz）不收敛，且构造在最小维度2上实现。

Abstract: Whether the Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno (BFGS) method and its variants can fail to converge on smooth nonconvex functions under realistic line-search parameters has remained a central open problem in quasi-Newton theory since the landmark counterexample of Dai (2002), which was confined to small Armijo parameters ($c_1 \le 1/84 \approx 0.0119$ on Powell's geometry, or $c_1 \le 69/7480 \approx 0.0092$ on his six-point cycle) and an objective function unbounded below. In this paper, we resolve the long-standing open question, posed by Dai (2002) following a discussion with J. C. Gilbert, of whether such counterexamples exist in theory for every Armijo parameter $c_1 \in (0, 1)$. Specifically, for every prescribed pair of line-search parameters $0 < c_1 < c_2 < 1$, we construct an objective function $f \in C^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$, bounded below and with Lipschitz continuous gradient, on which every method in a broad conjugacy class $\mathcal{C}$ equipped with the first-local-minimizer line search generates an infinite sequence of iterates with $\|\nabla f(x_k)\| = 1$ for all $k \ge 0$. The class $\mathcal{C}$ encompasses the classical full-memory BFGS method, limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) with arbitrary memory $m \ge 1$, the Broyden positive family, and the Hestenes-Stiefel conjugate gradient method. The steps are the standard first local minimizers along the search rays and simultaneously satisfy the strong Wolfe, weak Wolfe, Armijo, and Goldstein conditions with constants $(c_1, c_2)$. The construction operates in the minimal possible dimension $n = 2$, exploiting a non-decaying conjugate descent orbit in the plane coupled with an explicit tubular interpolation whose two-bump axial curvature profile places the Armijo ratio anywhere in $(0, 1)$.

</details>


### [168] [Heavy-Ball Method under Randomized Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09743)
*Chang He,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 通过预设随机参数调度，加速heavy-ball方法在一般光滑凸目标上的收敛，达到O(1/K^{3/2})的last-iterate速率，首次在多项式上超过经典O(1/K)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典heavy-ball方法在一般光滑凸函数上仅有O(1/K)收敛率，作者希望借助预设的随机参数调度（时间调度）实现更快收敛，并探索随机化程度对加速效果的影响。

Method: 区分两个随机化层次：(1) 在确定性边界区间内随机采样梯度评估时刻；(2) 额外随机化时间边界本身。分别构造固定时间与anytime调度，并进行期望与几乎必然的收敛分析。

Result: 确定性时间边界下，固定时间与anytime调度达到期望last-iterate函数值间隙O(1/K^{4/3})，anytime调度几乎必然同速率；随机化时间边界后，期望与几乎必然均提升至O(1/K^{3/2})。

Conclusion: 这是heavy-ball方法在一般光滑凸函数上首个全局非渐近、多项式优于O(1/K)的收敛保证，且速率严格优于plain gradient descent配合silver stepsize的最佳已知O(1/K^{log_2(1+√2)})。

Abstract: We study how predefined randomized parameter schedules accelerate the heavy-ball method on general smooth convex objectives. Our analysis distinguishes two levels of randomization: sampling gradient-evaluation times within intervals whose boundaries are deterministic, and additionally randomizing the time boundaries themselves. With deterministic time boundaries, we construct fixed-time and anytime schedules that achieve an expected last-iterate function value gap of order $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{4/3})$; the anytime schedule also satisfies the same rate almost surely. We then use randomized time boundaries and obtain the improved last-iterate rate $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{3/2})$, both in expectation and almost surely. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first global nonasymptotic convergence guarantee for the heavy-ball method on general smooth convex functions that improves polynomially over the classical $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$ rate. Our result shows that the heavy-ball method achieves a strictly better convergence rate than the best known result $\mathcal{O}(1/K^{\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})$ attainable by plain gradient descent with silver stepsize schedules.

</details>


### [169] [Closed-loop $α$-Potential Stochastic Differential Games via a BSDE Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09756)
*Xun Li,Liangquan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭环α-势随机微分博弈，用BSDE方法给出参数α的精确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前开环控制研究，需刻画闭环情形下α-势的近似误差，并理解反馈项对估计的影响。

Method: 采用倒向随机微分方程(BSDE)方法，引入一阶与二阶灵敏度状态过程以及控制过程灵敏度。

Result: 在N人异质主体均值场博弈中，得到最小势近似误差的N-一致上界；闭环界含随N不消失的反馈贡献。

Conclusion: 该估计一般不能保证α→0，闭环与开环估计存在本质差异。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the closed-loop $α$-potential stochastic differential game (SDG) problem as a continuation of our prior research on open-loop control (see \cite{GLZ2025}). By utilizing the backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) approach, we derive a precise estimate for the parameter $α$. Compared to our earlier work \cite{GLZ2025}, this study incorporates both first- and second-order sensitivity state processes, as well as the sensitivity of the control process. A distinguishing feature of this work is that, in the context of $N$-player heterogeneous agent games involving mean-field type interactions, we derive an $N$-uniform upper bound for the minimal potential approximation error. In contrast to the corresponding open-loop estimates, the closed-loop bound contains feedback-induced contributions that need not vanish with $N$. Consequently, our present estimate does not in general guarantee $α\rightarrow 0$.

</details>


### [170] [Arithmetic Scar Accessibility and Generic Schrodinger Control on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09758)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限紧致度量图上自由薛定谔方程的内部精确可控性，给出疤痕可达性的算术判据，并证明在有理独立（几乎处处）度量下GGCC是自由情形精确可控性的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图度量（长度向量）如何影响高频本征函数的质量集中（疤痕）与精确可控性，并厘清图几何控制条件（GGCC）在自由薛定谔方程中是否既必要又充分。

Method: 对给定原始环或外部到外部路径，将割点集的规则疤痕层与目标长度向量的轨道闭包环面相交，构造可被高频本征函数精确逼近的疤痕；在轨道闭包流满足内在Diophantine条件下做定量估计，并把谱结果与已知的GGCC图论刻画相结合。

Result: 给出疤痕可达性的算术判据，该判据可对有理相关之外的共振度量成立；Diophantine条件下沿本征序列，指定支撑外的质量多项式衰减，得到有限频率观测的多项式退化律。有理独立度量具有满相位轨道闭包，故所有原始障碍可达。

Conclusion: 对每个有理独立度量（因而对Lebesgue几乎每个度量），GGCC是自由薛定谔方程在任意正时刻精确可控性的充要条件。

Abstract: We study internal exact controllability of the free Schrödinger equation on finite compact metric graphs with Kirchhoff conditions at interior vertices and a fixed Dirichlet-Neumann assignment at exterior vertices. For a prescribed primitive cycle or exterior-to-exterior path, we identify an arithmetic criterion ensuring that the corresponding scar is accessible by exact high-frequency eigenfunctions of a fixed target metric. The criterion is formulated by intersecting the regular scar stratum of the secular set with the orbit-closure torus of the target length vector, and it can hold for resonant metrics beyond the rationally independent class. Under an intrinsic Diophantine condition on the orbit-closure flow, the construction is quantitative: along an exact eigensequence the mass outside the prescribed support decays polynomially, yielding a polynomial degeneration law for finite-frequency observation when that support is uncontrolled. Rationally independent metrics have full phase-orbit closure and therefore make every primitive obstruction accessible. Combining this spectral result with the known graph-theoretic characterization and sufficiency of the Graph Geometric Control Condition (GGCC), we prove that, for every rationally independent and hence for Lebesgue-almost every metric, GGCC is necessary and sufficient for exact controllability at every positive time in the free setting.

</details>


### [171] [MPFA: A Pareto Front Approximation Method for Riemannian Bi-objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09857)
*Kaiping Liu,P. -A. Absil,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Hermite插值的黎曼流形上光滑双目标优化的Pareto前沿逼近方法（MPFA），无需多个初始点即可生成连续近似Pareto前沿，并给出收敛性与逼近误差分析，应用于稀疏PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼流形多目标优化数值算法通常需要多个初始点，且难以直接生成连续的近似Pareto前沿；作者希望克服这些限制，实现单起点、连续且高精度的前沿逼近。

Method: 针对黎曼流形上的光滑双目标优化问题，利用Hermite插值技术构造Pareto前沿逼近（MPFA），在无需多个初始点的条件下生成连续的近似Pareto前沿；理论上建立方法收敛性并分析所得Pareto前沿的逼近误差。

Result: 在多个测试问题上的数值实验表明，该方法能以较高精度和合理计算代价有效逼近Pareto前沿；并将其应用于稀疏主成分分析的双目标形式，验证了在数据分析问题中的实际适用性。

Conclusion: MPFA为黎曼流形光滑双目标优化提供了一种无需多初始点、可生成连续Pareto前沿且具理论保证的高效逼近方法，并在稀疏PCA等实际应用中展现出潜力。

Abstract: We propose a Pareto front approximation (MPFA) method for smooth bi-objective optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds based on a Hermite interpolation technique. Compared with the existing multiobjective optimization numerical algorithms, the proposed method can generate a continuous approximate Pareto front without multiple initial points. We establish convergence of the proposed method and analyze the approximation error of the resulting Pareto front. Numerical experiments on several test problems demonstrate that the proposed approach can effectively approximate the Pareto front with high accuracy and reasonable computational cost. Furthermore, the method is applied to a bi-objective formulation of sparse principal component analysis, illustrating its practical applicability in data analysis problems.

</details>


### [172] [The Global Asymptotic Stability Problem for Linear MPC Is Undecidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09930)
*Johan Löfberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了带约束的有限时域线性模型预测控制（MPC）的全局渐近稳定性判定问题是不可判定的，即使在时域为1、权重为单位矩阵、优化器唯一且全局可行的最简情形下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清 MPC 稳定性不可判定性的根源：此前人们可能认为不可判定性源于长时域、维度增长、递归可行性失效或优化非唯一等复杂因素，作者希望证明这些都不是必要条件。

Method: 通过构造多组归约（reduction）来证明不可判定性，分别覆盖预测状态盒约束、硬输入盒约束以及二次软化的输入盒约束；另有一组归约将状态和输入维度分别固定为3和6。

Result: 在时域为1、状态/输入/终端权重均为单位矩阵、优化器唯一、全局可行的条件下，判定全局渐近稳定性不可判定；且对多种约束类型均成立，并可在固定低维（状态3维、输入6维）情形下成立。

Conclusion: MPC 全局渐近稳定的不可判定性并非由长时域、维度增长、递归可行性失效或非唯一性引起，而是内在的，即使在极简设定下也存在。

Abstract: We prove that deciding global asymptotic stability for constrained finite-horizon linear model predictive control is undecidable. This holds at horizon one with identity state, input, and terminal weights, unique optimizers, and global feasibility. Separate reductions cover predicted-state boxes, hard input boxes, and quadratically softened input boxes. A fourth reduction fixes the state and input dimensions to three and six. Hence undecidability is not caused by long horizons, growing dimensions, failures of recursive feasibility, or nonuniqueness.

</details>


### [173] [Boundary control for optimal mixing by two-dimensional second-grade fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09946)
*Kush Kinra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究二阶流体中非扩散标量的最优边界混合控制，控制量为Navier-slip边界切向牵引力，目标为终端混合范数，证明适定性、最优控制存在性与一阶变分不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非扩散标量在二维不可压缩二阶流体中的最优边界混合问题，借助Navier-slip边界下的切向牵引力实现终端混合范数最小化，并兼顾控制代价与enstrophy奖励。

Method: 以Navier-slip边界切向牵引力为控制变量；经非齐次边界提升后，$\mathbf{g}$与$\partial_t\mathbf{g}$进入状态方程。对被动标量证明全局适定性，对主动标量在控制无关的公共局部区间证明适定性；建立最优控制存在性、控制-状态映射的方向可微性与弱后向伴随公式。

Result: 证明最优控制存在；控制-状态映射在终端目标所需拓扑下方向可微；得到弱后向伴随公式及由对偶恒等式导出的一阶变分不等式。当$\beta=\zeta=0$时，额外伴随正则性给出充分大控制惩罚$\gamma$下最优控制的唯一性。

Conclusion: 为二阶流体边界混合控制建立了完整的适定性与最优性理论，并给出大惩罚下最优控制的唯一性条件。

Abstract: We study optimal boundary mixing of a nondiffusive scalar transported by a two-dimensional incompressible second-grade fluid. The control is the tangential traction in a Navier-slip boundary condition, and the principal objective is the terminal $(H^1(Ω))'$ mix-norm, supplemented by a quadratic control cost and an optional enstrophy reward. The second-grade constitutive law introduces a spatially filtered acceleration and a generalized vorticity; after lifting the nonhomogeneous boundary data, both $\mathbf{g}$ and $\partial_t\mathbf{g}$ enter the state equation, where $\mathbf{g}$ is the boundary control variable. We prove global state well-posedness for a passive scalar and well-posedness on a common, control-independent local interval for an active scalar. We then establish existence of an optimal control, directional differentiability of the control-to-state map in the topology required by the terminal objective, and a weak backward adjoint formulation. A duality identity yields the first-order variational inequality. In the absence of the friction coefficient $β$ and enstrophy reward weight $ζ$ (that is, $β=ζ=0$), the additional adjoint regularity implies uniqueness of the optimal control for all sufficiently large control penalties $γ$.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Verified Answers: Solver-Informed Self-Distillation for Bootstrapping Operations Research Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09957)
*Rui Zhu,Minglong Cao,Chenyu Zhou,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出SOLID框架，利用求解器工件反馈进行无验证答案、无外部评估器的自蒸馏，提升LLM运筹学建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于OR建模训练的三大局限：依赖人工/强模型验证的合成数据、信用分配粗糙或昂贵、特权自蒸馏导致部署时风格不匹配。

Method: SOLID执行多个rollout的候选程序，聚类其目标值，选择多数群体工件作为伪参考，再用组相对优势和密集自监督信号更新模型。

Result: 在多个OR基准上，SOLID对通用及OR微调模型的求解准确率均优于仅结果奖励的组相对训练。

Conclusion: 求解器工件可作为可扩展的自改进监督源，无需可信答案或外部评估器。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language descriptions into operations research (OR) formulations. Post-training techniques including reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation have further improved this capability. However, three limitations remain in training LLMs for OR formulations. First, training commonly relies on synthetic formulations validated by human experts or stronger models, constraining scalable supervision. Second, credit assignment is either coarse or costly: outcome rewards score an entire trajectory without locating the responsible modeling decision, whereas process-level supervision requires an additional evaluator. Third, privileged self-distillation can induce style mismatch by using solver context unavailable at deployment. We find that a model can improve from solver-artifact feedback generated by its own rollouts, making self-distillation a practical, evaluator-free source of dense supervision. Therefore, we propose SOLID: Solver-Informed On-Policy LearnIng through Self-Distillation, a novel framework for self-improving OR language models without verified answers or external evaluators. SOLID executes candidate programs from multiple rollouts, clusters their objectives, and selects a majority-group artifact as a pseudo-reference. The model then performs updates using group-relative advantages and dense self-supervision signals. Across multiple OR benchmarks, SOLID improves solution accuracy for both general-purpose and OR-tuned models over outcome-only group-relative training. These results show that solver artifacts can support scalable self-improvement without trusted answers.

</details>


### [175] [Null-controllability for some linear parabolic systems with controls acting on different parts of the domain and its boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09959)
*Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究常系数线性抛物型系统在多个分布/边界控制下的零可控性，证明一维情形下的Kalman秩条件，并在仅有分布控制时给出Carleman估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性抛物型系统的零可控性通常针对单一控制，而多控制作用于不同分布子域和/或边界的情形缺乏系统的秩条件判据，需要建立相应理论。

Method: 针对常系数线性抛物型系统，考虑多个控制分别作用于不同分布子域或边界；采用Kalman秩条件分析一维情形，并借助Carleman估计处理仅有分布控制的情况。

Result: 在一维情形下证明了Kalman秩条件；当仅考虑分布控制时，还建立了Carleman估计等相关结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kalman秩条件推广到多控制（分布/边界）抛物型系统的一维零可控性问题，并通过Carleman估计提供了分析工具。

Abstract: In this work we study the null-controllability properties of linear parabolic systems with constant coefficients in the case where several controls are acting on different distributed subdomains and/or on the boundary. We prove a Kalman rank condition in the one-dimensional case. In the case where only distributed controls are considered we also establish related results such as a Carleman estimate.

</details>


### [176] [Boundary approximate controllability of some linear parabolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09982)
*Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究两类线性抛物系统的边界近似能控性：n个热方程耦合系统与2×2级联系统，给出充分条件（任意维），并证明在1维、单控制器时为必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨线性抛物系统在边界控制下的近似能控性，针对耦合热方程和级联系统这两类重要模型，刻画其可控性条件，并对比分布控制与边界控制的差异。

Method: 基于H.O. Fattorini提出的一般性刻画方法，对每个系统推导充分条件；在1维且仅有一个控制时证明条件的必要性；对矩形区域上的耦合热方程，按控制域位置给出特征刻画。

Result: 获得任意维下的充分条件；证明1维单控制情形下该条件亦为必要；对矩形域耦合热方程给出依赖控制域位置的刻画；构造出一个分布能控但边界不能控的级联系统反例。

Conclusion: Fattorini的一般刻画可用于统一处理两类抛物系统的边界近似能控性，并揭示了边界控制与分布控制在级联系统中可能存在本质差异。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the boundary approximate controllability of two classes of linear parabolic systems, namely a system of $n$ heat equations coupled through constant terms and a $2 \times 2$ cascade system coupled by means of a first order partial differential operator with space-dependent coefficients. For each system we prove a sufficient condition in any space dimension and we show that this condition turns out to be also necessary in one dimension with only one control. For the system of coupled heat equations we also study the problem on rectangle, and we give characterizations depending on the position of the control domain. Finally, we exhibit a cascade system for which the distributed controllability holds whereas the boundary controllability does not. The method relies on a general characterization due to H.O. Fattorini.

</details>


### [177] [Observability and controllability for the Schrödinger equation on polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10096)
*Wei Qu,Zhiwen Duan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究多面体上Schrödinger方程的能观性与可控性，在初始数据具有H^s(s>d/2)正则性时，以边界的奇异集为控制区域，证明了能观性与可控性成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多面体边界存在奇异集，其上的Schrödinger方程分析与控制问题具有挑战性；且控制区域与特征函数集中区域一致，需要刻画相应的能观性。

Method: 采用Burq–Zworski黑箱控制的思路，通过半经典测度与多面体中台球流的动力性质，证明能观性预解估计；再用标准HUM论证由能观性推出可控性。

Result: 对凸多面体上的Schrödinger方程，当初始数据具有H^s(s>d/2)正则性时，以边界奇异集的任意非空开邻域为控制区域，能观性与可控性成立。

Conclusion: 验证了多面体上Schrödinger方程在奇异集附近控制的有效性，建立了能观性预解估计，并借助HUM得到可控性。

Abstract: We consider the observability and controllability for the Schrödinger equation on polyhedra. We show that for the Schrödinger equation on convex polyhedra in $\mathbb{R}^d\,(d\geq2)$, when the initial data possess $H^s\,(s>d/2)$ regularity, observability and controllability hold, with the control region being an arbitrary nonempty open neighborhood of the singular set of the boundary, which is the same as the control region for eigenfunctions. Our proof of observability is a concrete adaptation of the method in the Burq--Zworski black box control work, where the observability inequality is proved via a resolvent estimate. Hence, before proving observability, we present an observability resolvent estimate on polyhedra. The idea of the proof of this resolvent estimate comes from the case of eigenfunction concentration on polyhedra considered by Cekić--Georgiev--Mukherjee, which uses semiclassical measures and relies closely on the dynamical properties of the billiard flow in polyhedra. Finally, by a standard HUM argument, we show that observability implies controllability.

</details>


### [178] [A Robust Binary Nonlinear Solver for Multi-stage Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10145)
*Kashif Rashid*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多阶段决策框架，用于通用化地表述各类序贯决策问题，并设计了一种结合蒙特卡洛搜索与增量概率强化学习的鲁棒随机求解方法，以处理由此产生的二进制非线性优化问题，并给出了示例应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯决策问题形式多样，缺乏通用表述框架；同时该类问题常归结为二进制非线性优化，难以高效稳健求解。

Method: 提出多阶段决策框架以通用方式建模各类序贯决策问题；针对其导出的二进制非线性优化问题，采用蒙特卡洛搜索结合带强化机制的增量概率学习作为鲁棒随机求解方法。

Result: 通过一个示例应用验证了所提框架与求解方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 多阶段决策框架与基于蒙特卡洛搜索和增量概率强化学习的求解方法相结合，可有效处理二进制非线性序贯决策问题。

Abstract: This document describes two developments. The first is a Multi-stage Decision Framework that permits the formulation of various sequential decision-making problems in a generic manner. The second concerns a robust stochastic solution procedure designed to handle the binary nonlinear optimization problem stemming from the proposed decision framework. The method adopts Monte-Carlo search with incremental probabilistic learning with reinforcement. The two developments are described herein along with a demonstrative application example.
  Key words: Multi-stage, decisions, binary, nonlinear systems, probabilistic and reinforcement learning

</details>


### [179] [Distributed Vector Assembly via Selector-Anchored Diffusion with Local Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10201)
*Diana Vieira Fernandes,Carlos Santos Silva,Soummya Kar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出D+LI分布式向量装配原语，用选择子、局部创新与拉普拉斯扩散从独立权威块构造完整局部副本，并给出收敛与稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在独立拥有的权威数据块上构造完整局部副本，需在分散所有权下隔离并传播贡献；纯平均加原始加性选择子源无法做到这一点。

Method: 正交选择子保块身份，局部创新锚定各所有者贡献，拉普拉斯扩散在整个网络传播；误差算子逐坐标分解为恒等减所有者接地拉普拉斯。

Result: 全局收缩因子与总维度和块维度无关；时变外部目标下得到有限T的ISS界及Lipschitz协调映射（含非势）的自洽估计-控制增益条件；半代数势问题中证明KL有限长度收敛。

Conclusion: 三个数值实验验证收缩紧性、有限时域跟踪、可容许KL时间尺度及离散/非势外部映射。

Abstract: We introduce Selector-Anchored Diffusion with Local Innovation (D+LI), a distributed vector-assembly primitive that constructs complete local replicas from independently owned authoritative blocks. Orthogonal selectors preserve block identity, local innovation anchors each owner's contribution, and Laplacian diffusion propagates these contributions across the network. We show that pure averaging with raw additive selector sources cannot isolate this replica. The D+LI error operator decomposes coordinatewise as the identity minus an owner-grounded Laplacian, yielding global contraction with a factor independent of the total and block dimensions. For time-varying outer targets, we establish a finite-T input-to-state stability (ISS) bound and a self-consistent estimator-controller gain condition for Lipschitz coordination maps, including non-potential ones. For semi-algebraic potential problems, we prove Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) finite-length convergence of the coupled decision-assembly iteration with a fixed finite communication budget per outer iteration under explicit timescale-separation conditions. Three numerical experiments demonstrate contraction tightness and finite-horizon tracking, verify an admissible KL timescale, and illustrate discrete and non-potential outer maps.

</details>


### [180] [Circuit-Embedded Feeder-State Reconstruction for Low-Voltage Distribution Networks under Extreme Measurement Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10229)
*Diana Vieira Fernandes,Carlos Santos Silva,Soummya Kar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于物理的增广电路重构框架，在极端稀疏信息条件下重建低压配电网内部状态，并在459节点葡萄牙馈线上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低压配电网需要内部状态信息，但实时遥测通常仅有馈线首端有功和无功功率，下游需求与发电仅由静态合同元数据描述，信息极度稀疏。

Method: 利用馈线首端量测、匹配元数据与类别级用电曲线确定母线级负荷和发电先验；用这些先验参数化经阻抗支路耦合到物理馈线的辅助恒功率元件，通过求解非线性交流电路平衡完成状态重构；增广网络物理将稀疏信息映射为符合基尔霍夫定律的物理母线电压、相角和潮流，并用加权最小二乘（WLS）通过加权残差拟合重构状态。

Result: 在真实459节点葡萄牙低压馈线模型、672个15分钟快照的样本内校准合成基准上，所有快照均收敛；电压RMSE、MAE和最大绝对误差分别为0.0033、0.0014和0.0337 p.u.，RMSE低于两个独立设定的静态先验WLS基线；直接先验交流潮流校验的电压幅值高度一致，表明增广电路忠实实现了所构建的先验。

Conclusion: 该增广电路重构框架能在极端稀疏信息下以物理一致方式有效重构低压配电网状态，并优于静态先验基线。

Abstract: Low-voltage (LV) distribution feeders increasingly require internal state information, yet real-time telemetry is often limited to feeder-head active and reactive power, while downstream demand and production are described only by static contract metadata. This paper proposes a physics-based augmented-circuit reconstruction framework for this extreme-sparsity regime. Feeder-head measurements, matched metadata, and class-level profiles first determine bus-level load and production priors. These priors parameterize auxiliary constant-power elements coupled to the physical feeder through impedance branches, and the feeder state is reconstructed by solving the resulting nonlinear AC circuit equilibrium. Thus, the augmented-network physics maps the sparse information set into Kirchhoff-consistent physical-bus voltages, angles, and flows, whereas weighted least squares (WLS) reconstructs the state through weighted residual fitting. The method is evaluated on a real 459-bus Portuguese LV feeder model using an in-sample-calibrated synthetic benchmark of 672 fifteen-minute snapshots. It converged for every snapshot and achieved voltage RMSE, MAE, and maximum absolute error of 0.0033, 0.0014, and 0.0337 p.u., respectively. Its RMSE was lower than those of two independently specified static-prior WLS baselines. A direct-prior AC power-flow check produced closely matching voltage magnitudes, confirming that the augmented circuit faithfully realizes the constructed priors.

</details>


### [181] [An $O(1/T^3)$ algorithm for minimizing convex quadratic functions over the $L_1$ ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10314)
*Yuyuan Ouyang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究单位L1球上的凸二次最小化问题，提出首个达到O(L/T^3)收敛率的一阶算法，对应一阶oracle复杂度O((L/ε)^{1/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已有算法的最佳收敛率为O(L/T^2)，作者希望从信息复杂度理论角度突破这一限制，追求更快的收敛速度。

Method: 提出一种新一阶算法，将单位L1球内向量分量分解为"好"和"坏"部分，对坏部分使用对称秩1（SR1）更新；假设子问题可精确求解。算法灵感来自调和分析中的弱型L1估计与好坏分解思想。

Result: 在T次梯度评估后目标值误差为O(L/T^3)，是首个达到O((L/ε)^{1/3})一阶oracle复杂度的算法，但当前版本不一定实用。

Conclusion: 该算法从理论上超越了现有O(L/T^2)的收敛率，虽暂不实用，但为未来可实现的O(L/T^3)算法提供了方向。

Abstract: We study convex quadratic minimization over the unit $L_1$ ball in which the maximum eigenvalue of the Hessian matrix is bounded by a positive constant $L$. We propose a novel first-order algorithm with objective value error bounded by $O(L/T^3)$ after $T$ gradient evaluations, assuming that the subproblems involved in the algorithm can be solved exactly. To the best of our knowledge, the best convergence rate of algorithms in the literature is $O(L/T^2)$. From the perspective of information-based complexity theory, our proposed algorithm is the first in the literature that achieves the $O((L/\varepsilon)^{1/3})$ first-order oracle complexity, although its current version is not necessarily practical for implementation. We hope that our proposed algorithm could shed some light on future implementable and efficient $O(L/T^3)$-convergence-rate algorithms. The proposed algorithm incorporates a decomposition of components of vectors in the unit $L_1$-norm ball to "good" and "bad" parts, and uses symmetric rank-1 (SR1) updates on the bad parts. The proposed algorithm was developed after the author instructed the OpenAI ChatGPT 6 (Astra) model to study the problem using ideas of weak-type $L^1$ estimates and good-bad part decomposition in harmonic analysis and a recent result.

</details>


### [182] [Convergence of a Randomized Newton Method in Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10465)
*Edward Huynh,Björn Engquist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了一种带状态相关高斯噪声的随机牛顿优化算法，用于在非凸目标函数的有界区域内定位唯一全局最小点，并证明了全局几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机牛顿方法在一般非凸目标函数上定位唯一全局最小点的全局收敛性，填补带状态相关方差噪声牛顿算法的理论分析。

Method: 将牛顿算法与状态相关的加性高斯噪声耦合，在有界区域内进行收敛性分析，证明依赖算法的两个关键性质：非退化的探索性（保证有限步内进入最小点邻域）与噪声衰减性（高概率收缩并防止无限次离开该邻域）。

Result: 在有界区域设定下证明了该随机牛顿算法的全局几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 探索性与噪声衰减性共同保证了算法进入并保持在最小点邻域，从而实现全局几乎必然收敛。

Abstract: We analyze a stochastic Newton optimization scheme for locating the unique global minimizer of a general nonconvex objective function. The method couples a Newton algorithm to additive Gaussian noise with state-dependent variance. In the bounded domain setting, we prove global almost sure convergence. The proof is based on two features of the algorithm: a nondegenerate exploratory property that ensures entrance into a neighborhood of the minimizer after a finite number of steps, and a decaying-noise property that yields contraction with high probability and prevents infinitely many exits from the neighborhood of the minimum.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Two Margins in Difference-in-Differences with a Continuous Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09488)
*Fangzhou Yu*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究具有交错采纳和连续时不变剂量（dose）的DID，将每个队列-时间比较分解为"水平边际"和"响应边际"，并给出估计、联合推断及协变量调整扩展；在水利压裂应用中，两边际结论不同，OLS系数主要由水平边际驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续剂量的交错DID中，OLS系数混杂了不同来源的效应，缺乏对其因果分解的清晰理解。作者希望区分"实现剂量下的平均处理效应（水平）"与"结果变化对剂量的组内斜率（响应）"两个边际。

Method: 将每个队列-时间比较分解为水平边际（在水平平行趋势下等于处理队列与未处理对照的水平对比）和响应边际（组内结果变化对剂量的斜率，需响应平行趋势与选择收益限制）。证明连续剂量OLS系数是响应指数与单位平均剂量水平对比的凸组合，权重取决于未处理份额。提供两边际的估计量、跨队列-时间及事件时间聚合的联合推断，以及协变量调整扩展。

Result: 在水利压裂应用中，水平边际的联合零约束被拒绝，而响应指数则不被拒绝；连续剂量OLS系数主要来自水平边际。

Conclusion: 应将水平边际与响应边际分别报告，以避免连续剂量OLS系数掩盖不同因果来源的异质性效应。

Abstract: This paper studies difference-in-differences with staggered adoption and a continuous, time-invariant dose. Each cohort-time comparison contains two margins. The level margin is the average treatment effect at realized doses. Under level parallel trends it equals the level contrast between the treated cohort and not-yet-treated controls. The response margin is the within-cohort slope of the outcome change on dose. It uses no controls, and its causal interpretation requires a response parallel trends assumption and a restriction on selection on gains. We show that the continuous-dose OLS coefficient in each cohort-time comparison is a convex combination of the response index and the level contrast per unit of mean dose, with a mixing weight that depends on the not-yet-treated share. We provide estimators of both margins, joint inference across cohort-time comparisons and event-time aggregates, and a covariate-adjusted extension. In an application to hydraulic fracturing, the level leads reject a joint zero restriction, whereas the response-index leads do not. The continuous-dose OLS coefficient draws primarily on the level margin. We report the level and response margin separately.

</details>


### [184] [When is statistical evidence strong enough? Using hypothesis tests to value data collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09544)
*Aristotelis Epanomeritakis,Davide Viviano*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文把统计显著性重新表述为"立即给出政策建议"还是"等待更多证据"的选择，提出与p值并列汇报的弃权值（A-value），以识别最需要补充数据的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统假设检验只给出"显著/不显著"的二元结论，却忽视了决策的机会成本：立即推荐政策可能出错，而推迟决策又需承担等待与额外实验的代价。作者希望为"何时应弃权并继续收集证据"提供福利经济学基础。

Method: 在最小最大后悔（minimax regret）框架下，将福利最优决策刻画为一个统计检验，其显著性水平取决于补充证据的成本与精度；通过反演该规则定义A值——即基于初始证据弃权并建议进一步实验的盈亏平衡福利成本；并研究实验能力受限时的优先级排序规则。

Result: 福利最优规则等价于一个水平可调的检验；A值越大表示补充数据带来的边际福利改善越高。当实验容量有限时，优先在A值最大的情形收集额外数据可获得有限样本（finite-sample）福利保证。

Conclusion: 建议在报告p值的同时报告A值，以指导数据收集的优先级；该框架对经济项目评估等政策场景具有直接应用价值，并在补充数据能力受限时提供理论上的福利保障。

Abstract: We recast statistical significance as a choice between making an immediate policy recommendation and deferring it until further evidence is collected. We show that the welfare-optimal decision corresponds, under minimax regret, to a statistical test whose level depends on the cost and precision of additional evidence. Inverting this rule, we introduce and recommend reporting the abstention-value (A-value) alongside traditional p-values to determine where additional data collection is most needed. The A-value defines the break-even welfare cost of abstaining and recommending further experimentation given the initial evidence. When experimentation capacity is limited, prioritizing additional data collection where A-values are the largest yields finite-sample welfare guarantees. We illustrate its implications for economic program evaluation.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [The Log S-fBM model: Statistical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09405)
*Othmane Zarhali,Emmanuel Bacry,Jean-François Muzy*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文对Log S-fBM随机波动率模型进行统计分析，推导S-fBM及其积分波动率的标度性质，给出对H和间歇性敏感的尾部偏差不等式，并构建Hurst指数的假设检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Log S-fBM模型能调和粗糙波动率（H≈0.1）与多重分形波动率（H→0），且数值显示金融资产间歇性约0.02，需小间歇性近似进行GMM校准，但缺乏系统的统计理论分析。

Method: 推导S-fBM过程的标度性质与Log S-fBM积分波动率测度的标度性质；建立尾部分布对H和间歇性敏感的偏差不等式；针对零Hurst指数构建假设检验；并通过显式小间歇性公式重新审视对数波动率增量的标度不变性。

Result: 得到S-fBM与积分波动率测度的标度性质；给出敏感于H和间歇性的偏差不等式及尾部分布；建立区分粗糙与多重分形动态的Hurst指数检验；用显式小间歇性公式复现两种情形下的标度不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作为Log S-fBM模型提供了统计理论基础，包括偏差不等式和Hurst指数检验，可用于区分粗糙与多重分形波动率动态。

Abstract: The Log S-fBM model, introduced by Wu et al., is a stochastic volatility model whose log volatility is a stationary fractional Brownian motion (S-fBM): a stationary Gaussian process with power-decaying autocovariance driven by the Hurst exponent $H$, and variance scaled by an intermittency coefficient. A key property is that it reconciles rough volatility, where $H$ is typically near $0.1$ (see Gatheral et al.), with multifractal volatility, where $H$ is close to $0$ as in Bacry, Muzy et al.: the model's volatility measure converges to a multifractal random measure as $H\to0$. Numerical findings in Wu et al. show intermittency of order $0.02$ across financial assets, motivating a small intermittency approximation of log volatility moments for calibration via the general method of moments (GMM). In this work, we conduct a statistical analysis of the Log S-fBM model. We derive scaling properties of the S-fBM process and the Log S-fBM integrated volatility measure, present deviation inequalities with tail distributions sensitive to $H$ and intermittency, and develop a hypothesis test for the null Hurst exponent, i.e.\ rough versus multifractal dynamics. Finally, we revisit scale invariance of the log volatility increment process via explicit small-intermittency formulas, reproducing analogous properties in both regimes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率波框架，用广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程建模自适应智能体集体行为，并通过中国股市交易数据验证LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说，进而建议将纠缠博弈模块引入AGI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统新古典金融假设理性个体独立决策，但现实中人类智能（如交易行为）具有纠缠与自适应特征；同时传统ANN-based AI依赖海量不透明参数，缺乏类脑机制的可解释性与效率。

Method: 基于广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程构建概率波框架，推导可检验的本征模态，并用中国股市日内交易数据对自适应纠缠博弈模态进行实证分析。

Result: 自适应纠缠博弈模态解释82-94%（总体89%）的决策模式；2-12%的行为表现为对日内新闻、事件和环境的自适应，具有双平衡态和突变参考点；纯独立模式占比不足5%，与新古典金融预测形成鲜明对比，支持LCA假说。

Conclusion: 观察到的交易行为反映底层脑机制与内在智能决策，支持LCA假说；应将自适应纠缠博弈模块纳入AGI架构，将ANN-based AI与概率波纠缠脑模拟结合，发展类脑的人类处理单元（HPUs），以实现更紧凑、高效、鲁棒的AGI，尤其适用于具身智能与机器人。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation.
