Conclusion: 该结果将Davis-Kahan定理推广到子采样设定，支持大规模对称矩阵的可扩展谱分析。

Abstract: The Davis-Kahan theorem is a fundamental tool in spectral analysis, providing quantitative control over the distance between the eigenspaces of a symmetric matrix and its perturbation. However, when the matrix dimension is large, computing leading eigenvectors is computationally expensive, limiting the practical use of spectral methods in modern large-scale applications. This paper addresses this problem by proposing an independent Bernoulli sampling scheme and proves that the leading left singular vectors of the subsampled matrix faithfully approximate the target subspace of a low-rank symmetric matrix. Our main result is a subsampled Davis-Kahan bound that gives an explicit error bound depending directly on the sampling probability. The bound reveals the trade-off: the computational cost scales linearly with the sampling probability, while the statistical error scales as the inverse square root of the sampling probability. Our result thus extends the Davis-Kahan theorem to the subsampled setting, enabling scalable spectral analysis of large-scale symmetric matrices.

</details>


### [261] [Critical initialization destabilizes higher input derivatives in wide scalar-input networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09244)
*Prashant Singh,Pranav Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究宽随机初始化网络在临界（edge-of-chaos）条件下高阶输入导数的传播规律，推导至三阶的均值场递推关系，并给出残差网络的统一有界性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有edge-of-chaos理论主要关注一阶输入扰动的保持，但物理信息损失、score matching与导数正则化依赖高阶输入导数，因此需要理解二阶及以上导数在无限宽、临界初始化下的行为。

Method: 对光滑标量输入全连接网络，利用有限导数jet在无限宽极限、固定深度下的联合高斯性，推导至三阶的均值场递推；在方差不动点处精确，有限深度修正几何衰减。对分支缩放为L^{-1/2}的残差网络，在正则性假设下证明任意固定有限导数阶的方差一致有界。

Result: 临界时一阶导数方差与深度无关，而二阶导数方差在激活函数具有非零曲率时随深度线性增长；三阶系统在均值场susceptibilities上闭合。数值模拟验证了临界增长律、残差界与闭合递推。

Conclusion: 结果刻画的是初始化性质而非训练后网络性能；揭示了临界初始化下高阶导数方差随深度的增长机制，并表明残差连接可抑制该增长。

Abstract: The edge-of-chaos condition preserves first-order input perturbations in wide randomly initialized networks, but physics-informed losses, score matching and derivative regularization depend on higher input derivatives. For smooth scalar-input fully connected networks, using a joint Gaussianity of the finite derivative jet that holds in the infinite-width limit at each fixed depth, we derive mean-field recursions through third order that are exact at the variance fixed point, with finite-depth corrections that decay geometrically. At criticality, the first-derivative variance is depth-invariant, whereas the second-derivative variance grows linearly whenever the activation has nonzero curvature. The resulting third-order system closes on mean-field susceptibilities. For residual networks with branch scale L^{-1/2}, we prove that every fixed finite derivative order has uniformly bounded variance under explicit regularity assumptions. Simulations verify the critical growth laws, the residual bound, and the closed recursion. The results concern initialization, not trained-network performance.

</details>


### [262] [What Fixed-Rollout pass@k Evaluations Can Identify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09245)
*Pranav Singh,Prashant Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文证明在固定采样预算 n 下，pass@k 仅当 k≤n 时可识别，超出 n 的外推、尾部指数与常数在非参数意义下不可识别，并给出精确构造、识别区间与报告标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样评估中常用 pass@k 外推远超每问题采样数 n 的范围，但此类外推缺乏非参数可识别性依据，需要明确固定深度计数实验所丢失的信息。

Method: 在 pooled/random-task 条件二项模型下，用矩识别分析固定 n 成功计数；构造保持计数律但外推不兼容的分布；利用 Hausdorff 主表示计算紧的总体识别区间；并给出有限任务置信证书。

Result: 直接 pass@k（k≤n）可识别，k>n 的外推不可识别；在 Brown 等公开 10,000 rollout 数据上，n=16 时 k=1000 的失败率模糊度达 1.5 至 2600 倍；校准表明中间尺度失败占比不决定区间宽度。

Conclusion: 结果不否定参数化推理时扩展律，而提供评估其假设的非参数基线，并提出区分直接估计、识别集与模型条件预测的报告标准。

Abstract: Repeated-sampling evaluations increasingly extrapolate pass@k far beyond the number n of samples collected per problem. We show that, in the pooled/random-task conditional-Binomial model, fixed-n success counts identify only the n free moments of the latent per-task success distribution. Consequently, direct pass@k is identified for k <= n, but generic extrapolated pass@k, tail exponents, and tail constants are not identified for k > n, even with arbitrarily many exchangeable tasks at the same rollout budget. This is stronger than the observation that the usual estimator is undefined beyond n: it characterizes the information missing from the fixed-depth count-law experiment. We give exact count-law-preserving constructions with incompatible extrapolations, state the exceptional unique-extension case, and compute sharp population identified intervals through Hausdorff principal representations. On the public 10,000-rollout-per-problem release of Brown et al., counterfactual n = 16 evaluations leave failure at k = 1000 ambiguous by factors from 1.5 to over 2,600 across four MATH/GSM8K/CodeContests configurations. The calibration shows that intermediate-scale failure share alone does not determine width. Our result does not reject parametric inference-time scaling laws; it supplies the nonparametric baseline against which their assumptions can be evaluated. We give an exact, conservative one-coordinate finite-task confidence certificate and a reporting standard separating direct estimates, identified sets, and model-conditioned forecasts.

</details>


### [263] [CAST: Canonical Approximate Schur Tree for Approximate Cholesky on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09255)
*Meher Chaitanya,Cameron Musco,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出 CAST 及 CAST-ρ 方法，用加权随机生成树替代 Cholesky 消元中的稠密 Schur 补团，构建无偏、连通的稀疏近似因子，并分析其方差与成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据工作负载常需求解系数矩阵相同的多个 Laplacian/SDDM 系统，近似 Cholesky 预条件子的构造成本可被摊薄；但消元时枢轴会在 d 个活跃邻居间产生稠密 Schur 补团，带来填充与成本问题。

Method: CAST 用从该团中直接采样的加权随机生成树替换 Schur 补团，每次实现连通且恰含 d-1 条边，按树包含概率倒数重加权使更新无偏；分布与邻居顺序无关，并证明其杠杆分数边际在无偏逆边际单树估计中最小化最大归一化重加权边贡献。CAST-ρ 将每个邻居复制 ρ 份、各带 1/ρ 权重份额，在扩展团上采样加权生成树后再收缩，更新仍无偏连通，可在 O(ρd) 时间精确采样，且归一化局部 Schur 误差二阶矩满足 1/ρ 界。

Result: CAST 的杠杆分数边际具有最优性（最小化最大归一化重加权边贡献）；CAST-ρ 满足 1/ρ 的二阶矩界，增大 ρ 可降低认证的局部采样方差，但可能增加构造成本与后续填充。实验观察表明 CAST-1 是更快的默认选择，而 CAST-2 在其额外边贡献保持低廉时更优。

Conclusion: CAST 系列以随机生成树替代稠密团，在保持无偏与连通的同时控制局部采样方差；实践中建议默认用 CAST-1，当额外边成本低廉时改用 CAST-2。

Abstract: Graph-data workloads such as diffusion estimation, ranking, semi-supervised learning, and network optimization often solve many Laplacian or symmetric diagonally dominant M-matrix (SDDM) systems with the same coefficient matrix. Approximate Cholesky preconditioners eliminate vertices one at a time and store the resulting sparse approximate factorization, the \emph{factor}, whose construction cost is amortized across these solves. But eliminating a vertex, the \emph{pivot}, creates a dense Schur-complement clique among its $d$ active neighbors. We introduce CAST (Canonical Approximate Schur Tree), which replaces this clique with a weighted random spanning tree sampled directly from it. Every realization is connected and contains exactly d-1 edges, while reweighting each selected edge by the reciprocal of its tree-inclusion probability makes the update unbiased. The distribution is independent of the ordering of the pivot neighbors, and we prove that its leverage-score marginals minimize the largest normalized reweighted-edge contribution among unbiased inverse-marginal one-tree estimators. We also introduce CAST-$ρ$, which replaces each pivot neighbor with $ρ$ copies, each carrying a $1/ρ$ share of that neighbor's incident weight, samples a weighted random spanning tree on the expanded clique, and contracts the copies back to the original neighborhood. The resulting update remains unbiased and connected, can be sampled exactly in $O(ρd)$ time, and satisfies a $1/ρ$ bound on the second moment of the normalized local Schur error. Increasing $ρ$ therefore reduces certified local sampling variability, but may increase construction cost and downstream fill. Empirically, we observe that CAST-1 is the faster default, whereas CAST-2 is preferable when its additional edge contributions remain inexpensive.

</details>


### [264] [Tensor Network Moral Graph Recovery of Discrete Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09258)
*Á. Troyano Olivas,Chi-Hang Fred Fung,Hans H. Brunner,Momtchil Peev,Vicente Martin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出用全连接张量网络(FCTN)配合核范数正则化的键修正,从离散变量的概率分布中恢复因果DAG的道德图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从离散概率分布中直接恢复因果有向无环图(DAG)的道德图,利用张量网络结构刻画变量间的条件独立关系。

Method: 将每个键矩阵参数化为全1基线矩阵加低秩修正C_ij=U_ij V_ij^T,通过对因子的变分Frobenius范数惩罚实现核范数正则,驱使冗余键趋于零;有效图直接从优化后的键矩阵读出。

Result: 在忠实性、正定性及局部张量架构无隐式重路由假设下,证明零重构误差时每个最优FCTN的有效图恰等于道德图;对近似情形给出基于Fannes-Audenaert条件互信息连续性的恢复界及正则参数β的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该方法为从概率分布中恢复道德图提供了理论保证,并给出了近似情形下的显式恢复界和参数选择条件。

Abstract: We present a method for recovering the moral graph of a causal DAG from a probability distribution over discrete variables, using fully connected tensor networks (FCTNs) with nuclear-norm-regularized bond corrections. Each bond matrix is parameterized as a baseline all-ones matrix plus a low-rank correction $C_{ij} = U_{ij}V_{ij}^\top$, and the nuclear norm of the correction implemented via the variational Frobenius norm penalty on the factors drives unnecessary bonds to zero. We prove that under faithfulness, positivity, and a no-implicit-rerouting assumption on the local tensor architecture, \textbf{every} optimal FCTN with zero reconstruction error $\varepsilon = 0$ has effective graph exactly equal to the moral graph. For the approximate regime ($\varepsilon > 0$), we provide explicit recovery bounds using the Fannes-Audenaert continuity of conditional mutual information, and derive a sufficient condition on the regularization parameter $β$. The effective graph is read directly from the optimized bond matrices.

</details>


### [265] [Mode Coverage in Normalizing Flow Boltzmann Generators via Log-Ratio Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09473)
*Qi Feng,Rongjie Lai,Di Qi,Xuda Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出 log-ratio variation X_ω 与 KLXX 损失,用于 Boltzmann generator,改善模式覆盖并改进诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向 KL 训练依赖可能有偏或缺失模式的目标样本,导致流虽重要性权重显示高 ESS,却漏掉目标质量。

Method: 定义 log-ratio variation X_ω(加权测度下目标与推前对数密度比的平均绝对成对差),并构建 KLXX:在前向 KL 上加两个变分项(目标加权项提高精度,quench/temper 与 pushforward 混合样本项搜索候选模式)。推导 KLXX 的 Fisher-Rao 梯度流与 fixed-surrogate 误差界;用于自适应分阶段 Boltzmann generator,每阶段重加权,给出有界阶段权重下采样误差界并证明样本量渐近无偏。

Result: 数值实验中 KLXX 相比前向 KL 提升模式覆盖,改善每阶段诊断,恢复的观测量接近独立参考。

Conclusion: log-ratio variation 提供了前向 KL 通常忽略的信息。

Abstract: Normalizing flow Boltzmann generators retain a tractable pushforward density, but training with forward KL depends on target samples that may be biased or omit modes. As a result, a flow can miss target mass while its observed importance weights give a high effective sample size. We introduce the log-ratio variation $\X_ω$, the mean absolute pairwise difference of the target-to-pushforward log-density ratio under a weighting measure $ω$, and use it to define KLXX, a new loss function. Two log-ratio variations are added to the forward KL (denoted by the two X's): one weighted by the target to improve accuracy, the other by a mixture of quench and temper samples with pushforward samples to search candidate modes. We derive the Fisher--Rao gradient flow of KLXX, where both variations contribute nonpositive dissipation, and a fixed-surrogate error bound for KLXX. We use KLXX in an adaptive-staging Boltzmann generator, with importance reweighting at every stage. We bound the sampling error of its inference scheme when the stage weights are essentially bounded, and prove it asymptotically unbiased in the sample size. In the numerical tests, KLXX improves mode coverage over forward KL. It also improves the generator's per-stage diagnostics against the loss that built the schedule. The observables the generator recovers are close to independent references. The log-ratio variations thus supply information that the forward KL loss usually omits.

</details>


### [266] [Recovery Theory for Projected Power Iterations in Permutation Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09502)
*Vahan Huroyan,Gilad Lerman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏均匀噪声下的投影幂方法(PPM)用于多置换同步，在 log m = o(npπ₀²) 等条件下证明了单步精确恢复、块误差收缩轨迹以及端到端近精确恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对象置换同步在稀疏且存在均匀随机腐坏的观测模型下缺乏对PPM的精确理论刻画，尤其是单步恢复、整条迭代轨迹的收敛以及部分置换等推广情形。

Method: 以投影幂方法为核心工具，在每对观测概率p、正确概率π₀的稀疏均匀腐坏模型下分析：证明单步块级精确恢复；建立高概率事件下的块误差收缩（收缩因子O(m/(npπ₀²))与误差地板）；推广到独立非同分布的置换值腐坏，并用参考块谱初始化实现端到端保证。

Result: 在log m=o(npπ₀²)下证明每个指定块的单步精确恢复；在np≥C₀log n且m=o(npπ₀²)下证明误差收缩因子为O(m/(npπ₀²))、误差地板为O(e^{-cnpπ₀}+e^{-cnpπ₀²}+log n/n)，使任一盆地内估计经一次更新即达到消失块误差且后续迭代近乎精确。谱初始化器对齐块误差为O_P(m/(npπ₀²))，给出端到端近精确恢复。

Conclusion: PPM在稀疏均匀腐坏下具有强鲁棒性与快速收敛性，理论可精确迁移到共同支撑的部分置换；对变化支撑建立了确定性与概率性的共可见边际，并在更强全块信号条件下实现有限步精确恢复。

Abstract: We study the projected power method (PPM) for synchronizing \(n\) unknown permutations of \(m\) objects under a possibly sparse uniform corruption model. Each pair is observed with probability \(p\), and an observed measurement is uncorrupted with probability \(π_0\) and is otherwise an independent uniform permutation. Under \(\log m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove exact one-step recovery (with high probability) of each prescribed block for an independent estimate with a fixed positive majority of correct blocks. When \(np\ge C_0\log n\) and \(m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove that one high-probability event yields a block-error contraction simultaneously for every estimate whose optimally aligned error is at most \(0.5-ε\). The contraction factor is \(O(m/(npπ_0^2))\) and the error floor is \(O(e^{-cnpπ_0}+e^{-cnpπ_0^2}+{\log n}/{n})\). Consequently, one update maps every possibly data-dependent estimate in this basin to vanishing block error, and all subsequent iterates remain almost exact uniformly over the iteration index. The one-step and trajectory results extend to independent, non-identically distributed, permutation-valued corruptions with mean \(m^{-1} \mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\). Under the uniform model, a reference-block spectral initializer has aligned block error \(O_{\mathbb P}(m/(npπ_0^2))\), yielding an end-to-end almost-exact recovery guarantee. Under a stronger all-block signal condition, PPM reaches exact recovery after finitely many iterations. The theory transfers exactly to partial permutations with common support; for varying supports, we establish deterministic and probabilistic co-visibility margins.

</details>


### [267] [High-probability guarantees for linear accessibility in feature superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09556)
*Enrico Vompa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将神经网络特征的线性可及性建模为压缩感知问题，证明在次高斯噪声下所需维度随稀疏度线性增长（d=O(k log m)），而非此前的二次限制，并量化了线性表示假设的几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可通过特征叠加编码比维度更多的概念，但跨特征干扰限制了同时激活特征的线性可及性。作者希望厘清这种干扰带来的维度需求本质。

Method: 将线性可及性形式化为压缩感知问题，针对固定支撑集在次高斯噪声下推导高概率界，并用高斯尾近似在不同系统参数下验证这些界。

Result: 证明了充分维度随稀疏度线性缩放（d=O_ε(k log m)），优于此前最坏情况下的二次限制。

Conclusion: 该结果为线性表示假设的几何约束提供了量化，可作为评估稀疏自编码器、组合泛化与神经可解释性的框架。

Abstract: Neural networks can leverage feature superposition to encode more concepts than dimensions, but cross-feature interference constrains the linear accessibility of simultaneously active features. By framing linear accessibility as a compressed sensing problem, we derive high-probability bounds for fixed supports under subgaussian noise, proving the sufficient dimension scales linearly ($d=O_{\varepsilon}(k \log m)$) rather than prior worst-case quadratic limits. We then validate these bounds across system parameters through Gaussian-tail approximations. These results quantify the geometric constraints of the linear representation hypothesis, providing a framework for evaluating sparse autoencoders, compositional generalization, and neural interpretability.

</details>


### [268] [Learning with Synthetic Data via SGD in High-Dimensional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09572)
*Jichu li,Difan Zou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究合成数据对高维线性回归中单遍SGD泛化的影响，证明混合训练导致强模型崩溃，而两阶段训练可通过仅在首阶段使用合成数据避免这一性能下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可扩展模型训练，但可能引发强模型崩溃：任意固定比例的合成数据都会阻止性能随数据规模提升，留下无法消除的过高风险下限。作者希望厘清合成数据在何种训练协议下有害或有益。

Method: 在带模型偏移的高维线性回归中分析单遍SGD，建立混合训练与两阶段训练的有限样本风险界，将标准偏差方差与源不匹配效应（混合下的波动与持续漂移、两阶段下的滤波初始化偏差）分离；并在随机素描模型下推导两种协议的缩放律。

Result: 混合训练导致强模型崩溃，两阶段训练因仅在首阶段使用合成数据而避免风险下限；在优化饱和区给出混合训练的紧致缩放律，表明更大模型可能放大合成数据导致的退化；给出两阶段训练在相同真实数据预算下严格优于纯真实训练的确切有限样本充要条件。

Conclusion: 合成数据本身既非有害也非有益，其效果关键取决于数据质量与纳入合成数据的训练协议；简单的数据课程即可避免模型崩溃。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a promising way to scale model training beyond limited human-generated data but it may also induce strong model collapse (Dohmatob et al., 2024), where any fixed fraction of synthetic data prevents model performance from improving under data scaling, leaving a non-vanishing excess risk floor. In this paper, we study how synthetic data affects the generalization of one-pass SGD in high-dimensional linear regression with model shift. We establish finite-sample risk bounds for mixed and two-stage training, separating standard bias and variance from source-mismatch effects, namely fluctuation and persistent drift under mixing and filtered initialization bias under two-stage. These bounds reveal a sharp contrast: mixed training induces strong model collapse, while two-stage training avoids the floor by using synthetic data only in the first stage, showing that collapse is not inevitable under a simple data curriculum. Under a random sketch model, we further obtain scaling laws for both protocols, with tight results for mixed training in the optimization-saturated regime. These laws show that larger models may amplify synthetic-induced degradation under mixing, and quantify how high-quality synthetic pretraining may reduce bias in two-stage training. Finally, we establish an exact finite-sample necessary-and-sufficient condition for two-stage training to strictly outperform real-only training under the same real-data budget and identical real-stage updates. Overall, our results highlight that synthetic data is neither inherently harmful nor beneficial; its effect depends critically on both its quality and the training protocol used to incorporate it.

</details>


### [269] [Distillation of Synthetic Data for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09586)
*Niloy Biswas,Noureddine El Karoui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出合成数据蒸馏(SDD)，利用已知数据生成过程，将TSFM训练目标从对比实现值改为对比条件预测分布，相当于Rao-Blackwell化，可降低随机梯度协方差、加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型越来越多地在合成轨迹上预训练，此时数据生成过程已知。但现有预训练损失只把模型输出与每条轨迹实际实现的未来值对比，忽略了已知的条件预测分布信息，造成梯度噪声较大、收敛效率不高。

Method: 提出合成数据蒸馏(SDD)：训练损失不对比实现值，而对比每条轨迹的条件预测分布。理论上SDD是训练目标的Rao-Blackwell化——在Loewner偏序下保持随机梯度期望不变，同时可证明地降低随机梯度协方差。

Result: 在4M至2.5B参数的TSFM模型族上验证，所有模型规模下验证损失收敛更快；在高斯过程数据上，SDD达到或优于原损失且训练迭代次数减少10%–40%。

Conclusion: SDD通过利用已知的数据生成过程，在不改变梯度期望的前提下降低梯度方差，为TSFM预训练提供了更高效的损失设计，且在不同模型规模上具备一致的可扩展性。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pre-trained on synthetically generated time series trajectories, where the data generating process is known. Current pre-training recipes are based on loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to realized future values of each trajectory. We instead propose loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to the conditional forecast distribution of each trajectory, a procedure we call synthetic data distillation (SDD). SDD corresponds to a Rao-Blackwellization of the training objective, in that it leaves the expectation of stochastic gradients unchanged while provably reducing the covariance of the stochastic gradient under the Loewner partial ordering. We empirically validate SDD on a TSFM model family of sizes from $4$M to $2.5$B parameters, and observe faster convergence of validation loss at every model size: on Gaussian Process data, SDD attains or improves upon the Status Quo loss whilst requiring $10\%-40\%$ less training iterations.

</details>


### [270] [Why Learning Rediscovers the Closed-Form Diagonal Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09656)
*Jeahn Han,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出模态逆问题中的"对角饱和原理"：当截断噪声各向同性时，贝叶斯最优Tikhonov形状是先验决定的闭式幂律，与域无关；理论与FEM实验证明对角正则化器难以稳健超越该闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模态逆问题中正则化器的最优形状由什么决定，以及学习型（对角）正则化器相对闭式解的增益空间有多大——即"学习何时开始有用"。

Method: 利用Berry随机波猜想使截断噪声跨模态去相关，用Weyl特征值计数律保证模态足够多，从而推导出对角饱和原理；在FEM声学房间上用闭式解、逐房间oracle调参及三种对角架构进行对比，并通过已知指数型Green函数修正扩展到热扩散。

Result: 闭式解在观测窗口上接近逐房间oracle最优；三种对角架构在同一数据上训练，重建误差与闭式解相差1个百分点以内，尽管学到的谱在性质上不同；Learned Iterative Ridge通过利用跨模态耦合突破了该边界。

Conclusion: 对角饱和是模态逆问题的结构性限制：闭式幂律解近乎最优，学习型对角正则化器收益有限；学习只有在突破对角族、利用跨模态耦合时才开始显现增益。

Abstract: We identify a diagonal saturation principle in modal inverse problems: when truncation noise is isotropic, the Bayes-optimal Tikhonov shape is a closed-form power law Gamma_k proportional to lambda_k^|s| set by the prior alone, independent of the domain. Berry's random-wave conjecture decorrelates the truncation noise across modes, and Weyl's eigenvalue counting law supplies enough modes for the conclusion to survive empirical Berry violations. Together they predict an approximately flat loss landscape across the per-mode family, leaving narrow scope for a diagonal regularizer to robustly beat the closed form. On FEM-simulated acoustic rooms, the closed form is near-optimal relative to per-room oracle tuning across observation windows, and three diagonal architectures trained on the same data match its reconstruction error within 1 pp despite learning qualitatively different spectra. The framework extends to heat diffusion via a known exponential Green's function correction with no new free parameters. Saturation is restricted to the diagonal family: Learned Iterative Ridge crosses the boundary by exploiting cross-mode coupling, locating where learning starts to help.

</details>


### [271] [A Unifying Perspective on Probabilities as Model Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09855)
*Benedikt Höltgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"预测方法"（prediction method）框架，将所有概率视为依赖于抽象构建方式与预测转换方式的输出，统一了不同概率解释，并证明在有限校准准则满足时可预期效用分布、支持成功决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率陈述无处不在，但贝叶斯派与频率派等对概率的理解存在根本分歧；且何时、为何依据概率行动能带来理想结果尚不清楚。作者希望提供一个统一视角来调和这些分歧。

Method: 引入"预测方法"概念，即概率取决于特定抽象构建方式与其向预测的转换方式；据此分析不同种类概率的统一性，并结合归纳论证与概率演算，论证有限校准准则的可行性。

Result: 表明即使是所谓客观概率也依赖模型；证明当满足有限校准准则时，可预期给定策略的效用分布，并在有限事件集上支持成功决策；解释校准准则在许多情境下的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文发展了一个关于概率及其使用的连贯视角，将其他解释背后的关键直觉联系起来，为概率理解与决策应用提供统一框架。

Abstract: Although probabilistic statements are ubiquitous, foundational disagreements persist about their understanding, as exemplified by debates between Bayesians and frequentists; moreover, it is unclear when and why acting on them actually leads to desirable outcomes. Here, we argue that every probability is the output of a \emph{prediction method}, that is, it depends on both a particular way of constructing abstractions and a way of transforming them into predictions. Through this, we provide a unifying perspective on supposedly different kinds of probabilities and show that even supposedly objective ones are model-dependent. We demonstrate that when a finite calibration criterion is met, one can anticipate the distribution of utilities for a given policy and inform successful decision-making on finite sets of events. Based on the notion of prediction methods, inductive arguments, and the probability calculus, we explain the feasibility of the calibration criterion in many settings. Overall, we develop a coherent perspective on probabilities and their use, connecting key intuitions behind other interpretations along the way.

</details>


### [272] [FlowCPO: A Unified Divergence View of Preference Alignment for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09905)
*Yansen Han,Shengyi Liao,Peng Sun,Deyuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Tao Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出统一的散度框架组织流/扩散模型的偏好对齐方法，并提出离线前向KL目标FlowCPO，用偏好与非偏好样本进行对齐而无需在线采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流与扩散模型的偏好对齐分为在线强化学习和离线偏好优化两类，但二者关系不清晰。已有前向过程对齐方法需要当前模型的新样本，而基于固定偏好对的离线方法主要依赖仅正样本微调或DPO式似然比代理。

Method: 通过基于散度的框架统一现有方法，提出FlowCPO——一种离线前向KL目标，同时利用偏好和非偏好样本且无需在线rollout。在线性插值下，证明在显式正则条件下前向KL目标被对比流匹配损失所界定，从而在固定数据上得到可处理的代理损失。还证明该损失非负，而简化FlowDPO的有符号回归损失可能下界无界。

Result: 域内设置下，FlowCPO的平均GenEval和OCR分数高于基线，CFG 3.0时达0.84和0.87，而FlowDPO为0.81和0.74。域外设置结果混合：GenEval最佳，但在若干指标上奖励分数低于RFT。

Conclusion: FlowCPO为流模型提供了一种无需在线采样的离线偏好对齐方法，在域内表现优于基线，域外表现不均衡。

Abstract: Preference alignment for flow and diffusion models now spans online reinforcement learning and offline preference optimization, but the relation between these methods remains unclear. In particular, existing forward-process alignment methods require fresh samples from the current model, while offline methods based on fixed preference pairs rely primarily on positive-only fine-tuning or DPO-style likelihood-ratio surrogates. We organize these approaches through a divergence-based framework and introduce FlowCPO, an offline forward-KL objective that uses both preferred and dispreferred samples without online rollouts. For linear interpolation, we show under explicit regularity conditions that the forward-KL objective is bounded by a contrastive flow matching loss, yielding a tractable surrogate on fixed data. We further show that this loss is nonnegative, whereas the signed regression loss of simplified FlowDPO can be unbounded below. In the in-domain setting, FlowCPO achieves higher mean GenEval and OCR scores than the evaluated baselines, reaching 0.84 and 0.87 versus 0.81 and 0.74 for FlowDPO at CFG 3.0. In the out-of-domain setting, the results are mixed, with the best GenEval result but lower reward scores than RFT on several metrics.

</details>


### [273] [Optimal Value Inference for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09981)
*Nan Lu,Ethan Lee,James M. Robins,David Simchi-Levi,Junwei Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究强化学习中最优值的离线推断，通过自诱导Bellman方程构造扰动项，提出去偏估计并证明渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线推断最优值在RL中重要，但行为策略随时间变化、horizon发散等情形带来挑战，需要有效推断方法。

Method: 将最大Bellman算子用softmax对应近似，导出两个作为自诱导Bellman方程不动点的扰动项；基于Neyman正交性构造去偏估计，并给出具体估计流程。

Result: 在horizon发散及行为策略随时间变化下建立渐近正态性（要求扰动项达到机器学习方法可实现的统计率）；合成实验验证数值表现，并应用于共享单车调度与AI智能体工具使用。

Conclusion: 提出的去偏推断方法有效，能在实际决策问题中实现有效推断。

Abstract: We study offline inference for the optimal value in reinforcement learning. Two new nuisances are derived as fixed points of a self-induced Bellman equation, in which we approximate the maximum Bellman operator by its softmax correspondence. We propose a debiased estimator through the Neyman orthogonality and establish its asymptotic normality under diverging horizons even when the behavior policy changes with time, as long as the nuisances have the statistical rates that can be achieved by many machine learning methods. We provide a concrete estimating procedure for these nuisances and show they can lead to valid inference. Synthetic experiments validate the numerical performance of our inference method, and we implement it in real-life decision-making problems, including bike repositioning and AI agentic tool use.

</details>


### [274] [A statistical approach to bias in zero-shot learning: the lens of handwriting recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10084)
*Clarence Chew,Gim Siang Chia,Sukalpa Chanda,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Soumendu Sundar Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对广义零样本学习(GZSL)中因对已见类的偏置导致难以扩展到大量未见类的问题,提出一种统计式的偏置纠正方法,在手写词识别的大规模词表场景下验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GZSL方法对大量未见类的适用性有限,且存在对训练中已见类的严重误分类偏置,难以扩展到大规模词表场景。

Method: 将传统GZSL特征学习器视为黑盒,把偏置纠正类比为分布外推断问题。采用两阶段层次架构:第一阶段用经典GZSL黑盒,第二阶段用轻量级蒙特卡洛偏置纠正器集成;纠正后根据预测的训练状态(已见/未见)限制分类范围,并使用最近邻、逻辑回归、随机森林等统计方法进行分类。

Result: 相比已有技术,未见词的分类相对准确率提升超过20%;大规模词表下的词识别可用约15维的低维表示完成。

Conclusion: 该方法以即插即用方式与任意经典GZSL学习器结合,具有广泛的适用性;并配有数学分析支撑其统计偏置纠正思想。

Abstract: Generalized zero-shot learning (GZSL) has emerged as an important paradigm for visual recognition systems that must generalize to classes that were not observed during training. Traditional GZSL techniques are limited by their applicability to a relatively small number of such unseen classes, scalability beyond which is challenging due to its well-known misclassification bias towards classes observed during training. In this work, we investigate the GZSL paradigm through the lens of zero-shot handwritten word recognition over extremely large vocabularies. We propose a statistical approach to rectifying this bias, which views any classical GZSL feature learner as a black box mechanism whose intrinsic bias in identifying the training status (seen vs. unseen) of a typical data point we aim to correct, similar to an out of distribution inferential problem. Our method leverages a simple two-stage hierarchical architecture, combining a classical GZSL blackbox in the first stage and an ensemble of lightweight Monte Carlo bias-correctors in the second. Once debiased, the classification of test data is undertaken only restricted to its predicted training status via well-founded statistical methods (eg nearest neighbour, logistic regression and random forests). We achieve relative accuracy improvements of over 20% in the classification of unseen words compared to established techniques. A key outcome is that word recognition over large scale vocabularies is amenable to a much lower dimensional representation (~15 dimensions). Our approach is underpinned by mathematical analysis that captures the essence of the statistical approach to bias correction. Our approach to bias rectification can be combined in a turn-key fashion with any classical GZSL learner as a blackbox, thereby suggesting a wide scope of applicability of this method for a wide variety of GZSL implementations in different domains.

</details>
